<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 352. STATE RETIREMENT</h2>
      
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.001"><b>352.001</b> MS 2006 [Renumbered <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.001">15.001</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.01">
        <h1 class="shn">352.01 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Terms.</span></h2>
          <p>Unless the language or context clearly indicates that a different meaning is intended, the terms defined in this section, for the purposes of this chapter, have the meanings given them.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">State employee.</span></h2>
          <p>"State employee" means any employee or officer in the classified and unclassified service of the state. The term also includes the special classes of persons listed in subdivision 2a but excludes the special classes of persons listed in subdivision 2b.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Included employees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) "State employee" includes:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) employees of the Minnesota Historical Society;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) employees of the State Horticultural Society;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) employees of the Minnesota Crop Improvement Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) employees of the adjutant general whose salaries are paid from federal funds and who are not covered by any federal civilian employees retirement system;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) employees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities who are employed under the university or college activities program;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) currently contributing employees covered by the system who are temporarily employed by the legislature during a legislative session or any currently contributing employee employed for any special service as defined in subdivision 2b, clause (6);</p>
          <p class="in">(7) employees of the legislature who are appointed without a limit on the duration of their employment;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) trainees who are employed on a full-time established training program performing the duties of the classified position for which they will be eligible to receive immediate appointment at the completion of the training period;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) employees of the Minnesota Safety Council;</p>
          <p class="in">(10) any employees who are on authorized leave of absence from the Transit Operating Division of the former Metropolitan Transit Commission and who are employed by the labor organization which is the exclusive bargaining agent representing employees of the Transit Operating Division;</p>
          <p class="in">(11) employees of the Metropolitan Council, Metropolitan Parks and Open Space Commission, Metropolitan Sports Facilities Commission, or Metropolitan Mosquito Control Commission unless excluded under subdivision 2b or are covered by another public pension fund or plan under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/473.415#stat.473.415.3">473.415, subdivision 3</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(12) judges of the Tax Court;</p>
          <p class="in">(13) personnel who were employed on June 30, 1992, by the University of Minnesota in the management, operation, or maintenance of its heating plant facilities, whose employment transfers to an employer assuming operation of the heating plant facilities, so long as the person is employed at the University of Minnesota heating plant by that employer or by its successor organization;</p>
          <p class="in">(14) personnel who are employed as seasonal employees in the classified or unclassified service;</p>
          <p class="in">(15) persons who are employed by the Department of Commerce as a peace officer in the Commerce Fraud Bureau under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/45.0135">45.0135</a> who have attained the mandatory retirement age specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.34#stat.43A.34.4">43A.34, subdivision 4</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(16) employees of the University of Minnesota unless excluded under subdivision 2b, clause (3);</p>
          <p class="in">(17) employees of the Middle Management Association whose employment began after July 1, 2007, and to whom section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.029">352.029</a> does not apply;</p>
          <p class="in">(18) employees of the Minnesota Government Engineers Council to whom section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.029">352.029</a> does not apply;</p>
          <p>(19) employees of the Minnesota Sports Facilities Authority;</p>
          <p>(20) employees of the Minnesota Association of Professional Employees;</p>
          <p>(21) employees of the Minnesota State Retirement System;</p>
          <p>(22) employees of the State Agricultural Society;</p>
          <p>(23) employees of the Gillette Children's Hospital Board who were employed in the state unclassified service at the former Gillette Children's Hospital on March 28, 1974;</p>
          <p>(24) if approved for coverage by the Board of Directors of Conservation Corps Minnesota, employees of Conservation Corps Minnesota so employed on June 30, 2003; and</p>
          <p>(25) employees of the Perpich Center for Arts Education who are covered by the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System as of July 1, 2016.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Employees specified in paragraph (a), clause (13), are included employees under paragraph (a) if employer and employee contributions are made in a timely manner in the amounts required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04</a>. Employee contributions must be deducted from salary. Employer contributions are the sole obligation of the employer assuming operation of the University of Minnesota heating plant facilities or any successor organizations to that employer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.2b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.2b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Excluded employees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">"State employee" does not include:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) persons who are:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) students employed by the University of Minnesota, or within the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system, unless approved for coverage by the Board of Regents of the University of Minnesota or the Board of Trustees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities, whichever applies;</p>
          <p>(ii) employed as interns for a period not to exceed six months unless included under subdivision 2a, paragraph (a), clause (8);</p>
          <p>(iii) employed as trainee employees unless included under subdivision 2a, paragraph (a), clause (8); or</p>
          <p>(iv) employed in the student worker classification as designated by Minnesota Management and Budget;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) employees who are:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) eligible for membership in the state Teachers Retirement Association, unless the person is an employee of the Department of Education who elected to be covered by the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System instead of the Teachers Retirement Association;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) employees of the state who, in any year, were credited with 12 months of allowable service as a public school teacher and, as such, are members of a retirement plan governed by chapter 354 or 354A unless the employment is incidental employment as a state employee that is not covered by a retirement plan governed by chapter 354 or 354A;</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) employees of the state who are employed by the Board of Trustees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities in an unclassified position that is listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.08#stat.43A.08.1">43A.08, subdivision 1</a>, clause (9);</p>
          <p class="in">(iv) persons employed by the Board of Trustees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities who elected retirement coverage other than by the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System under Minnesota Statutes 1994, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136C.75">136C.75</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(v) officers or enlisted personnel in the National Guard or in the naval militia who are assigned to permanent peacetime duty and who are or are required to be members of a federal retirement system under federal law;</p>
          <p class="in">(vi) persons employed by the Department of Military Affairs as full-time firefighters and who, as such, are members of the public employees police and fire retirement plan;</p>
          <p class="in">(vii) members of the State Patrol retirement plan under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352B.011">352B.011</a>, subdivision 10;</p>
          <p class="in">(viii) off-duty police officers while employed by the Metropolitan Council and persons employed as full-time police officers by the Metropolitan Council and who, as such, are members of the public employees police and fire retirement plan; and</p>
          <p class="in">(ix) employees of the state who have elected to transfer account balances derived from state service to the unclassified state employees retirement program under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352D.02#stat.352D.02.1d">352D.02, subdivision 1d</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) employees of the University of Minnesota who are excluded from coverage by action of the Board of Regents;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) election judges and persons who are employed solely to administer elections;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) persons who are:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) engaged in public work for the state but who are employed by contractors when the performance of the contract is authorized by the legislature or other competent authority;</p>
          <p>(ii) employed to perform professional services where the service is incidental to the person's regular professional duties and where compensation is paid on a per diem basis; or</p>
          <p>(iii) compensated on a fee payment basis or as an independent contractor;</p>
          <p>(6) persons who are employed:</p>
          <p>(i) on a temporary basis by the house of representatives, the senate, or a legislative commission or agency under the jurisdiction of the Legislative Coordinating Commission;</p>
          <p>(ii) as a temporary employee on or after July 1 for a period ending on or before October 15 of that calendar year for the Minnesota State Agricultural Society or the Minnesota State Fair, or as an employee at any time for a special event held on the fairgrounds;</p>
          <p>(iii) by the executive branch as a temporary employee in the classified service or as an executive branch temporary employee in the unclassified service if appointed for a definite period not to exceed six months, and if employment is less than six months, then in any 12-month period;</p>
          <p>(iv) by the adjutant general if employed on an unlimited intermittent or temporary basis in the classified service or in the unclassified service for the support of Army or Air National Guard training facilities;</p>
          <p>(v) by a state or federal program for training or rehabilitation as a temporary employee if employed for a limited period from an area of economic distress and if other than a skilled or supervisory personnel position or other than a position that has civil service status covered by the retirement system; and</p>
          <p>(vi) by the Metropolitan Council or a statutory board of the Metropolitan Council where the members of the board are appointed by the Metropolitan Council as a temporary employee if the appointment does not exceed six months;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) receivers, jurors, notaries public, and court employees who are not in the judicial branch as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.02#stat.43A.02.25">43A.02, subdivision 25</a>, except referees and adjusters employed by the Department of Labor and Industry;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) patient and inmate help who perform services in state charitable, penal, and correctional institutions, including a Minnesota Veterans Home;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) employees of the Sibley House Association;</p>
          <p>(10) persons who are:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) members of any state board or commission who serve the state intermittently and are paid on a per diem basis, the secretary, secretary-treasurer, and treasurer of those boards if their compensation is $5,000 or less per year, or, if they are legally prohibited from serving more than three years, and the board of managers of the State Agricultural Society and its treasurer unless the treasurer is also its full-time secretary;</p>
          <p>(ii) examination monitors employed by a department, agency, commission, or board of the state to conduct examinations that are required by law; or</p>
          <p>(iii) appointees serving as a member of a fact-finding commission or an adjustment panel, an arbitrator, or a labor referee under chapter 179;</p>
          <p class="in">(11) emergency employees who are in the classified service, but if an emergency employee, within the same pay period, becomes a provisional or probationary employee on other than a temporary basis, the employee must be considered a "state employee" retroactively to the beginning of the pay period;</p>
          <p class="in">(12) persons who are members of a religious order who are excluded from coverage under the federal Old Age, Survivors, Disability, and Health Insurance Program for the performance of service as specified in United States Code, title 42, section 410(a)(8)(A), as amended, if no irrevocable election of coverage has been made under section 3121(r) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986, as amended;</p>
          <p class="in">(13) members of trades who are employed by the successor to the Metropolitan Waste Control Commission, who have trade union pension plan coverage under a collective bargaining agreement, and who are first employed after June 1, 1977;</p>
          <p class="in">(14) for the first three years of employment, foreign citizens who are state employees under subdivision 2 or included employees under subdivision 2a, unless the foreign citizen is:</p>
          <p class="in">(i) an H-1B, H-1B1, or E-3 status holder;</p>
          <p class="in">(ii) an employee legally authorized to work in the United States for three years or more; or</p>
          <p class="in">(iii) an employee otherwise required to participate under federal law; and</p>
          <p>(15) reemployed annuitants of the general state employees retirement plan, the military affairs personnel retirement plan, the transportation department pilots retirement plan, the state fire marshal employees retirement plan, or the correctional state employees retirement plan during the course of that reemployment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Head of department.</span></h2>
          <p>"Head of department" means the head of any department, institution, or branch of the state service that directly pays salaries out of its income or that prepares, approves, and submits salary abstracts of its employees to the commissioner of management and budget.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Accumulated contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>"Accumulated contributions" means the total, exclusive of interest, of (1) the sums deducted from the salary of an employee, (2) the amount of payments, including assessments, paid by the employee in lieu of salary deductions and all other payments made under this chapter and credited to the employee's individual account in the retirement fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Retirement fund.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Retirement fund" means the general state employees retirement fund created by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04#stat.352.04.1">352.04, subdivision 1</a>, with respect to the general state employees retirement plan or the correctional state employees retirement fund created by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.911#stat.352.911.1">352.911, subdivision 1</a>, with respect to the correctional state employees retirement plan.</p>
          <p>(b) The retirement fund includes the aggregate of accumulated contributions of employees covered by the applicable plan, and all other funds paid into the state treasury or received by the director under this chapter, together with all income and profits from the money and interest on it, including contributions on the part of the federal government, the state, and state departments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/57/#laws.0.29.0">Ex1967 c 57 s 29</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.7.1.0">1993 c 307 art 7 s 1</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.33.0">1957 c 928 s 33</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.33.0">1957 c 928 s 33</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Allowable service.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) "Allowable service" means:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) service by an employee for any calendar month in which the employee is paid salary from which deductions are made, deposited, and credited in the fund, including deductions made, deposited, and credited as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.041">352.041</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) service by an employee for any calendar month for which payments in lieu of salary deductions are made, deposited, and credited in the fund, as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.27">352.27</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) the period of absence from their duties by employees who are temporarily disabled because of injuries incurred in the performance of duties and for which disability the state is liable under the workers' compensation law until the date authorized by the director for the commencement of payments of a total and permanent disability benefit from the retirement fund;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) service covered by a refund repaid as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.23">352.23</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352D.05#stat.352D.05.4">352D.05, subdivision 4</a>, except service rendered as an employee of the adjutant general for which the person has credit with the federal civil service retirement system;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) service rendered before July 1, 1978, by an employee of the Transit Operating Division of the Metropolitan Transit Commission or by an employee on an authorized leave of absence from the Transit Operating Division of the Metropolitan Transit Commission who is employed by the labor organization which is the exclusive bargaining agent representing employees of the Transit Operating Division, which was credited by the Metropolitan Transit Commission-Transit Operating Division employees retirement fund or any of its predecessor plans or funds as past, intermediate, future, continuous, or allowable service as defined in the Metropolitan Transit Commission-Transit Operating Division employees retirement fund plan document in effect on December 31, 1977;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) service rendered after July 1, 1983, by an employee who is employed on a part-time basis for less than 50 percent of full time, for which the employee is paid salary from which deductions are made, deposited, and credited in the fund, including deductions made, deposited, and credited as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.041">352.041</a> or for which payments in lieu of salary deductions are made, deposited, and credited in the fund as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.27">352.27 </a>must be credited on a fractional basis either by pay period, monthly, or annually based on the relationship that the percentage of salary earned bears to a full-time salary, with any salary paid for the fractional service credited on the basis of the rate of salary applicable for a full-time pay period, month, or a full-time year. For periods of part-time service that is duplicated service credit, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.1">356.30, subdivision 1</a>, paragraphs (g) and (h), govern; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) any period of authorized leave of absence without pay that does not exceed one year and for which the employee obtained credit by payment to the fund under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.017">352.017</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) For purposes of paragraph (a), clauses (1) and (2), any salary that is paid for a fractional part of any calendar month, including the month of separation from state service, is deemed to be the compensation for the entire calendar month.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Allowable service determined and credited on a fractional basis must be used in calculating the amount of benefits payable, but service as determined on a fractional basis must not be used in determining the length of service required for eligibility for benefits.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Actuarial equivalent.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Actuarial equivalent" means the condition of one annuity or benefit having an equal actuarial present value as another annuity or benefit, determined as of a given date at a specified age with each actuarial present value based on the appropriate mortality table adopted by the board of directors based on the experience of the fund as recommended by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>, and approved under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.18">356.215, subdivision 18</a>, and using the applicable investment return assumption specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) For purposes of computing a joint and survivor annuity, the investment return assumption specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.461">356.461</a> must be used, rather than the investment return specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Salary.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Subject to the limitations of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.611">356.611</a>, "salary" means wages, or other periodic compensation, paid to an employee before deductions for deferred compensation, supplemental retirement plans, or other voluntary salary reduction programs.</p>
          <p>(b) "Salary" does not include:</p>
          <p>(1) lump-sum sick leave payments;</p>
          <p>(2) severance payments;</p>
          <p>(3) lump-sum annual leave payments and overtime payments made at the time of separation from state service;</p>
          <p>(4) payments in lieu of any employer-paid group insurance coverage, including the difference between single and family rates that may be paid to an employee with single coverage;</p>
          <p>(5) payments made as an employer-paid fringe benefit;</p>
          <p>(6) workers' compensation payments;</p>
          <p>(7) employer contributions to a deferred compensation or tax-sheltered annuity program; and</p>
          <p>(8) amounts contributed under a benevolent vacation and sick leave donation program.</p>
          <p>(c) Amounts paid to an employee by the employer through a grievance proceeding or a legal settlement are salary only if the grievance or settlement agreement is received by the executive director no fewer than 14 days before payment is made and the executive director determines that:</p>
          <p>(1) the grievance or settlement agreement describes with sufficient specificity the period or periods of time worked or not worked by the employee for which the amounts are compensation; and</p>
          <p>(2) the amounts are salary as defined in paragraph (a) and the determination is consistent with prior determinations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.13a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.13a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13a.<span class="headnote">Reduced salary during period of workers' compensation.</span></h2>
          <p>An employee on leave of absence receiving temporary workers' compensation payments and a reduced salary or no salary from the employer who is entitled to allowable service credit for the period of absence, may make payment to the fund for the difference between salary received, if any, and the salary the employee would normally receive if not on leave of absence during the period. The employee shall pay an amount equal to the employee and employer contribution rate under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3, on the differential salary amount for the period of the leave of absence.</p>
          <p>The employing department, at its option, may pay the employer amount on behalf of its employees. Payment made under this subdivision must include interest at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.2">356.59, subdivision 2</a>, and must be completed within one year of the return from leave of absence.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.14">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.14">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.14a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.14a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14a.<span class="headnote">Average salary.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Average salary" means the average of the highest five successive years of salary upon which the employee has made contributions to the retirement fund by payroll deductions. Average salary must be based upon all allowable service if this service is less than five years.</p>
          <p>(b) "Average salary" does not include the payment of accrued unused annual leave or overtime paid at time of final separation from state service if paid in a lump sum nor does it include the reduced salary, if any, paid during the period the employee is entitled to workers' compensation benefit payments for temporary disability.</p>
          <p>(c) For an employee covered by the correctional state employees retirement plan, "average salary" means the average of the monthly salary during the employee's highest five successive years of salary as an employee covered by the general state employees retirement plan, or the correctional state employees retirement plan, or by a combination of the two. If the total of the covered service is less than five years, the determination of average salary must be based on all allowable service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.15">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 15." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.15">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Approved actuary.</span></h2>
          <p>"Approved actuary" means an actuary who meets the definition in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.1">356.215, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.16">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 16." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.16">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Year of allowable service.</span></h2>
          <p>"Year of allowable service" means any 12 calendar months not necessarily consecutive in which an employee is entitled to allowable service credit. It also means 12 months credit each calendar year for teachers in the state universities and state institutions who may or may not receive compensation in every month in the calendar year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.17">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.17">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Total and permanent disability.</span></h2>
          <p>"Total and permanent disability" means the inability to engage in any substantial gainful activity by reason of any medically determinable physical or mental impairment that has existed or is expected to continue for a period of at least one year.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.17a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.17a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17a.<span class="headnote">Occupational disability.</span></h2>
          <p>"Occupational disability," for purposes of determining eligibility for disability benefits for a correctional employee, means a disabling condition that is expected to prevent the correctional employee, for a period of not less than 12 months, from performing the normal duties of the position held by the correctional employee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.17b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.17b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17b.<span class="headnote">Duty disability, physical or psychological.</span></h2>
          <p>"Duty disability, physical or psychological," for a correctional employee, means an occupational disability that is the direct result of an injury incurred during, or a disease arising out of, the performance of normal duties or the performance of less frequent duties either of which present inherent dangers specific to the correctional employee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.17c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.17c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17c.<span class="headnote">Regular disability, physical or psychological.</span></h2>
          <p>"Regular disability, physical or psychological," for a correctional employee, means an occupational disability resulting from a disease or an injury that arises from any activities while not at work or from activities while at work performing normal or less frequent duties that do not present inherent dangers specific to covered correctional positions.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.17d">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17d." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.17d">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17d.<span class="headnote">Normal duties.</span></h2>
          <p>"Normal duties" means specific tasks designated in the applicant's job description and which the applicant performs on a day-to-day basis, but do not include less frequent duties which may be requested to be done by the employer from time to time.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.17e">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 17e." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.17e">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17e.<span class="headnote">Less frequent duties.</span></h2>
          <p>"Less frequent duties" means tasks designated in the applicant's job description as either required from time to time or as assigned, but which are not carried out as part of the normal routine of the applicant's job.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.18">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 18." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.18">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">"Annuity" and "benefit" synonymous.</span></h2>
          <p>The words "annuity" and "benefit," wherever they appear in this chapter, are synonymous.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.19">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 19." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.19">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Retirement.</span></h2>
          <p>"Retirement" means the time after a state employee is entitled to an accrued annuity, as defined in subdivision 21, payable under an application for annuity filed in the office of the system as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.8">352.115, subdivision 8</a> or, in the case of an employee who has received a disability benefit, when that employee reaches normal retirement age.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.20">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 20." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.20">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Retired employee.</span></h2>
          <p>"Retired employee" means an employee who has retirement status as defined in subdivision 19.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.21">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 21." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.21">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Accrued annuities.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) In this chapter and chapters 3A, 352B, 352C, and 490, "accrued annuity" means an annuity that had become payable to a retired employee in the lifetime of the employee.</p>
          <p>(b) An annuity or benefit authorized as provided in this chapter and chapters 3A, 352B, 352C, and 490 becomes payable on the first day of each calendar month for that calendar month and must be paid on the first day of each calendar month.</p>
          <p>(c) Notwithstanding any provision to the contrary in this chapter and chapters 3A, 352B, 352C, and 490, benefit payment authorized as "payable for life" is payable for the entire month in which death occurs, and the benefit payment for the month of death is payable to the surviving spouse or other beneficiary only if the annuitant dies before negotiating the benefit check.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.22">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 22." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.22">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 22.<span class="headnote">Disabled employee.</span></h2>
          <p>"Disabled employee" means an employee who is totally and permanently disabled as defined in subdivision 17, and who as a result of the disability is entitled to receive a disability benefit as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113">352.113</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.23">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 23." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.23">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 23.<span class="headnote">Coverage or covered by the system.</span></h2>
          <p>"Coverage" or "covered by the system" means that a state employee who serves the state of Minnesota and makes the required employee contributions to the retirement fund is, by reason of these contributions, entitled to either (1) a retirement annuity, or (2) a disability benefit, or (3) a refund of accumulated contributions, as provided in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.24">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 24." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.24">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 24.<span class="headnote">System.</span></h2>
          <p>"System" means the Minnesota State Retirement System.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.25">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 25." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.25">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 25.<span class="headnote">Normal retirement age.</span></h2>
          <p>"Normal retirement age" means age 65 for a person who first became a covered employee or a member of a pension fund listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, before July 1, 1989. For a person who first becomes a covered employee after June 30, 1989, normal retirement age means the higher of age 65 or "retirement age," as defined in United States Code, title 42, section 416(l), as amended, but not to exceed age 66.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.26">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 26." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.26">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 26.<span class="headnote">Dependent child.</span></h2>
          <p>"Dependent child" means an individual who is a biological or adopted child of a deceased employee, has not reached the age of 20, and is dependent upon the employee for more than one-half of the child's support at the time of the employee's death. It also means a child of the member conceived during the member's lifetime and born after the member's death, unless a parent-child relationship does not exist under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/524.2-120#stat.524.2-120.10">524.2-120, subdivision 10</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.01.27">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 27." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.01.27">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 27.<span class="headnote">APRN.</span></h2>
          <p>"APRN" means an individual licensed as an advanced practice registered nurse by the Board of Nursing as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/148.171#stat.148.171.3">148.171, subdivision 3</a>, who provides services to a member or an applicant that are within the scope of the APRN's professional licensure.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
