<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/277/#laws.1.73.0">2008 c 277 art 1 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.1.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.2.3.0">2009 c 169 art 2 s 3</a>-8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.2.1.0">2010 c 359 art 2 s 1</a>; art 4 s 1; art 12 s 11; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/187/#laws.1.59.0">2012 c 187 art 1 s 59</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/299/#laws.1.8.0">2012 c 299 art 1 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.2.9.0">2013 c 111 art 2 s 9</a>; art 7 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/135/#laws.3.22.0">2013 c 135 art 3 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.1.1.0">2014 c 296 art 1 s 1</a>,2; art 4 s 2; ; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.2.2.0">2015 c 68 art 2 s 2</a>; art 11 s 1; art 13 s 1,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.2.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 2</a>; art 9 s 4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.8.23.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 8 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.5.1.0">2020 c 108 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2021/0/22/#laws.2.1.0">2021 c 22 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/65/#laws.5.1.0">2022 c 65 art 5 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.5.1.0">2024 c 102 art 5 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.015"><b>352.015</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1951/0/441/#laws.0.1.0">1951 c 441 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.33.0">1957 c 928 s 33</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.016">
        <h1 class="shn">352.016 UNIVERSITY OF MINNESOTA EMPLOYEES; FURLOUGH SERVICE AND SALARY CREDIT.</h1>
        <p>A furloughed employee of the University of Minnesota who is a member of the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System may obtain allowable service credit and salary credit for the furlough period. The allowable service and salary credit authorization under this section is a leave of absence authorization for purposes of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.017">352.017</a> and the purchase payment procedure of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.017#stat.352.017.2">352.017, subdivision 2</a>, applies.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.016.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.15.1.0">2010 c 359 art 15 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.017">
        <h1 class="shn">352.017 AUTHORIZED LEAVE OF ABSENCE SERVICE CREDIT PURCHASE PROCEDURE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.017.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.017.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Except for leaves or breaks in service covered by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.27">352.27</a>, this section applies to all plans specified in this chapter for any period of authorized leave of absence without pay that does not exceed one year and for which the employee obtains credit for allowable service by making payment as specified in this section to the applicable fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.017.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.017.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Purchase procedure.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An employee covered by a plan specified in this chapter may purchase credit for allowable service in that plan for a period specified in subdivision 1 if the employee makes a payment as specified in paragraph (b) or (c), whichever applies. The employing unit, at its option, may pay the employer portion of the amount specified in paragraph (b) on behalf of its employees.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If payment is received by the executive director within one year from the date the employee returned to work following the authorized leave, the payment amount is equal to the employee and employer contribution rates specified in law for the applicable plan at the end of the leave period multiplied by the employee's hourly rate of salary on the date of return from the leave of absence and by the days and months of the leave of absence for which the employee is eligible for allowable service credit. The payment must include compound interest at the applicable monthly rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.2">356.59, subdivision 2</a>, from the last day of the leave period until the last day of the month in which payment is received. If payment is received by the executive director after one year, the payment amount is the amount determined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.551">356.551</a>. Payment under this paragraph must be made before the date of termination from public employment covered under this chapter.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) If the employee terminates employment covered by this chapter during the leave or following the leave rather than returning to covered employment, payment must be received by the executive director within 30 days after the termination date. The payment amount is equal to the employee and employer contribution rates specified in law for the applicable plan on the day prior to the termination date, multiplied by the employee's hourly rate of salary on that date and by the days and months of the leave of absence prior to termination.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.017.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.5.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.2.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/86/#laws.1.64.0">2009 c 86 art 1 s 64</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.3.1.0">2015 c 68 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.3.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.02"><b>352.02</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.33.0">1957 c 928 s 33</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.021">
        <h1 class="shn">352.021 MINNESOTA STATE RETIREMENT SYSTEM.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.021.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.021.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) There is established the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System for state employees.</p>
          <p>(b) The general state employees retirement plan is a continuation of the State Employees Retirement Association.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.021.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.021.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">State employees covered.</span></h2>
          <p>Every person who becomes a state employee as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01">352.01</a> is covered by the general state employees retirement plan. Acceptance of state employment or continuance in state service is deemed to be consent to have deductions made from salary for deposit to the credit of the account of the state employee in the retirement fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.021.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.021.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Optional exemptions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any person who is appointed by the governor or lieutenant governor who is not already covered by the general state employees retirement plan under this chapter and who is not an employee listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352D.02#stat.352D.02.1">352D.02, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (c), may request, in writing, an exemption from coverage by the plan.</p>
          <p>(b) To qualify for this exemption, the request must be made within 90 days from the date of entering upon the duties of the position to which the person is appointed.</p>
          <p>(c) After making the request, a person requesting the exemption is not entitled to coverage by the general state employees retirement plan while employed in the position that entitled that person to an exemption from coverage.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.021.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.021.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>MS 1957 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/6/#laws.0.34.0">Ex1959 c 6 s 34</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.021.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.021.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Reentering service after refund.</span></h2>
          <p>When a former employee who has withdrawn accumulated contributions reenters employment in a position entitled to coverage under the general state employees retirement plan, the employee must be covered by the plan on the same basis as a new employee and is not entitled to allowable service credit for any former service. The annuity rights forfeited when taking a refund can only be restored as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.23">352.23</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.021.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.021.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>MS 1994 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/262/#laws.1.26.0">1995 c 262 art 1 s 26</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.021.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.021.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Determining applicable law.</span></h2>
          <p>An annuity under this chapter must be computed under the law in effect as of the last day for which the employee receives pay, or if on medical leave, the day that the leave terminates. However, if the employee has returned to covered employment following a termination, the employee must have earned at least six months of allowable service following a return to employment as a state employee in order to qualify for improved benefits resulting from any law change enacted subsequent to that termination.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.021.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.7.0">1957 c 928 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/6/#laws.0.5.0">Ex1959 c 6 s 5</a>,6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.9.0">1963 c 383 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/57/#laws.0.8.0">Ex1967 c 57 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/221/#laws.0.3.0">1973 c 221 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.4.0">1983 c 128 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.1.3.0">1989 c 319 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.10.2.0">1990 c 570 art 10 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.11.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 11</a>-14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.9.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.3.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 3</a>-5</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.022"><b>352.022</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/564/#laws.0.51.0">1984 c 564 s 51</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.028">
        <h1 class="shn">352.028 COVERAGE TERMINATION.</h1>
        <p>Coverage of any person under the system ends when the person ceases to be a state employee.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.028.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.10.0">1963 c 383 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/57/#laws.0.9.0">Ex1967 c 57 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.029">
        <h1 class="shn">352.029 COVERAGE FOR EMPLOYEES OF LABOR ORGANIZATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.029.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.029.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Qualifications.</span></h2>
          <p>Unless already specifically included under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2a">352.01, subdivision 2a</a>, or unless specifically excluded under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2b">352.01, subdivision 2b</a>, a state employee on leave of absence without pay to provide service as an employee or officer of a labor organization that is an exclusive bargaining agent representing state employees may elect under subdivision 2 to be covered by the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System for service with the labor organization, subject to the limitations set forth in subdivisions 2a and 2b.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.029.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.029.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Election.</span></h2>
          <p>A person described in subdivision 1 is covered by the system if written election to be covered is delivered to the executive director within 90 days of being employed by the labor organization, or within 90 days of starting the first leave of absence with an exclusive bargaining agent, whichever is later.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.029.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.029.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Limitations on salary for benefits and contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The covered salary for a labor organization employee who is a member under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01">352.01</a>, subdivision 2a, paragraph (a), or who qualifies for membership under this section or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.75">352.75</a> is limited to the lesser of:</p>
          <p>(1) the employee's actual salary as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.13">352.01, subdivision 13</a>; or</p>
          <p>(2) 75 percent of the salary of the governor as set under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15A.082">15A.082</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) The limited covered salary determined under this subdivision must be used in determining employee, employer, and employer additional contributions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3, and in determining retirement annuities and other benefits under this chapter and chapter 356.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.029.2b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.029.2b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Earning restrictions apply.</span></h2>
          <p>A retirement annuity is only payable, if the person has met any other applicable requirements, upon the termination of employment by the labor organization by the person who is a member under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01">352.01, subdivision </a>2a, paragraph (a), or who elected coverage under subdivision 1. The reemployed annuitant earnings limitation set forth in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.10">352.115, subdivision 10</a>, applies in the event that the person who is a member under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2a">352.01, subdivision 2a</a>, paragraph (a), or who elected coverage under subdivision 1 retires and is subsequently reemployed while an annuitant by the labor organization or by any other entity employing persons who are covered by the Minnesota State Retirement System by virtue of that employment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.029.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.029.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>The employee and employer contributions required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04</a>, or by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.92">352.92</a> for employees covered by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.91">352.91</a>, are the obligation of the employee who is a member under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2a">352.01, subdivision 2a</a>, paragraph (a), or who chooses coverage under this section. However, the employing labor organization may pay the employer contributions. Contributions made by the employee must be made by salary deduction. The employing labor organization shall pay all contributions to the system as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04</a>, or by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.92">352.92 </a>for employees covered by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.91">352.91</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.029.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.029.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/432/#laws.1.11.0">1992 c 432 art 1 s 11</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.029.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.029.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Board membership excluded.</span></h2>
          <p>Employees of a labor organization who become members of the system under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2a">352.01, subdivision 2a</a>, paragraph (a), or under this section are not eligible for election to the board of directors.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.029.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1977/0/429/#laws.0.13.0">1977 c 429 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.45.0">1981 c 224 s 45</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/7/#laws.0.5.0">1Sp1985 c 7 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.10.3.0">1990 c 570 art 10 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/432/#laws.1.2.0">1992 c 432 art 1 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.4.1.0">1994 c 528 art 4 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.7.2.0">2013 c 111 art 7 s 2</a>-6; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.6.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 6</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.03">
        <h1 class="shn">352.03 BOARD OF DIRECTORS; EXECUTIVE DIRECTOR.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Membership of board; election; term.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The policy-making function of the system is vested in a board of 11 members known as the board of directors. This board shall consist of:</p>
          <p>(1) three members appointed by the governor, one of whom must be a constitutional officer or appointed state official and two of whom must be public members knowledgeable in pension matters;</p>
          <p>(2) four state employees elected by active and inactive vested members of the general state employees retirement plan and members with an account balance in the unclassified employees retirement program, excluding employees and inactive vested members for whom a board member is designated under clause (3);</p>
          <p>(3) one employee of the Metropolitan Council's transit operations or its successor agency designated by the executive committee of the labor organization that is the exclusive bargaining agent representing employees of the transit division;</p>
          <p>(4) one employee who is a member of the State Patrol retirement plan elected by active and inactive vested members of that plan;</p>
          <p>(5) one employee who is a member of the correctional state employees retirement plan established under this chapter elected by active and inactive vested members of that plan; and</p>
          <p>(6) one retired employee of a plan included in the system, elected by disabled and retired employees of the plans administered by the system at a time and in a manner determined by the board.</p>
          <p>(b) The terms of the four elected state employees under paragraph (a), clause (2), must be staggered, with two of the state employee board positions elected each biennium, whose terms of office begin on the first Monday in May after their election. Elected members and the appointed member of the Metropolitan Council's transit operations hold office for a term of four years and until their successors are elected or appointed, and have qualified.</p>
          <p>(c) An employee or former employee of the system is not eligible for membership on the board of directors. A state employee on leave of absence is not eligible for election or reelection to membership on the board of directors.</p>
          <p>(d) The term of any board member who is on leave for more than six months automatically ends on expiration of the term of office.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Terms; compensation; removal; vacancies; public members.</span></h2>
          <p>The membership terms, compensation, removal of members, and filling of vacancies for the public members on the board are as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/15.0575">15.0575</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.1b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.1b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1b.<span class="headnote">Membership voting limitations.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Active members and inactive vested members of a plan under this chapter, chapter 352B, or chapter 352D are eligible to vote in board elections as further specified and restricted in this section.</p>
          <p>(b) Retired members and disabilitants from a plan in the system may vote only for the retired member position under subdivision 1, paragraph (a), clause (6).</p>
          <p>(c) If an inactive vested member of a plan under this chapter, chapter 352B, or chapter 352D is an inactive vested member of more than one plan covered by the system, that person is eligible to vote only in elections applicable for inactive vested members of the plan in the system from which the person last received allowable service.</p>
          <p>(d) If a person is an active member of a plan in the system and is an inactive vested member or a retiree from another plan or plans in the system, the person is only eligible to vote in board elections applicable due to the active member plan membership.</p>
          <p>(e) If a person is an inactive vested member from a plan in the system and is also a retiree from another plan in the system, the person is only eligible to vote in elections applicable due to the retiree status.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Vacancy.</span></h2>
          <p>Any vacancy of a state employee or retired employee in the board caused by death, resignation, or removal of any member so elected must be filled by the board for the unexpired portion of the term in which the vacancy occurs. Any vacancy of the employee of the transit operating division member of the board caused by death, resignation, or removal must be filled by the governing board of the labor organization that is the exclusive bargaining agent representing employees of the Transit Operating Division.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1976/0/134/#laws.0.79.0">1976 c 134 s 79</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Duties and powers of board of directors.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The board shall:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) elect a chair;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) appoint an executive director;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) establish rules to administer this chapter and chapters 3A, 352B, 352C, 352D, and 490 and transact the business of the system, subject to the limitations of law;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) consider and dispose of, or take any other action the board of directors deems appropriate concerning, denials of applications for annuities or disability benefits under this chapter, chapter 3A, 352B, 352C, 352D, or 490, and complaints of employees and others pertaining to the retirement of employees and the operation of the system;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) oversee the administration of the deferred compensation plan established in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.965">352.965</a>;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) oversee the administration of the health care savings plan established in section 352.98; and</p>
          <p class="in">(7) approve early retirement and optional annuity factors for all plans administered by the system, including approving retirement annuity factors for the unclassified state employees program under chapter 352D, subject to review by the actuary retained by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement; establish the schedule for implementation of the approved factors; and notify the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement of the implementation schedule.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The board shall advise the director on any matters relating to the system and carrying out functions and purposes of this chapter. The board's advice shall control.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Additional duties of the board.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may consider, review, and make recommendations regarding the financial and other needs of retired employees and may disseminate appropriate retirement information to the retired employee. Notwithstanding laws to the contrary, the board, at its discretion, may supply the names and addresses of retirees who were employed by the University of Minnesota at the time of termination to the University of Minnesota and state agencies for retirees who were employed by the specific state agency at the time of termination. The board, at its discretion, may supply names and addresses of state and university retirees to an organization that has been in existence for at least ten years and represents over 5,000 retired state and university employees. The names and addresses of each retiree can only be given to this organization once within 60 days of the effective date of the annuity. The board shall require the retiree organization, University of Minnesota, or applicable state agency to reimburse the fund for any administrative expense of providing the list. The list remains the property of the Minnesota State Retirement System and may not be subsequently sold, conveyed, given, or otherwise transferred by the retiree organization, the University of Minnesota, or the state agency to a third party. Periodically, retirees must be given an opportunity to specify that their name and address not be distributed under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Executive director, deputy director, and assistant director.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The board shall appoint an executive director, in this chapter called the director, on the basis of education, experience in the retirement field, ability to manage and lead system staff, and ability to assist the board in setting a vision for the system. The director must have had at least five years' experience in either an executive level management position or in a position with responsibility for the governance, management, or administration of a retirement plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The executive director, deputy director, and assistant director must be in the unclassified service but appointees may be selected from civil service lists if desired. Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, the board must set the salary of the executive director. The board must review the performance of the executive director on an annual basis and may grant salary adjustments as a result of the review. The salary of the deputy director and assistant director must be set in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/43A.18#stat.43A.18.3">43A.18, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Duties and powers of executive director.</span></h2>
          <p>The management of the system is vested in the director, who is the executive and administrative head of the system. The director may appoint a deputy director and an assistant director with the approval of the board. The director shall be advisor to the board on matters pertaining to the system and shall also act as the secretary of the board. The director shall:</p>
          <p>(1) attend meetings of the board;</p>
          <p>(2) prepare and recommend to the board appropriate rules to carry out this chapter;</p>
          <p>(3) establish and maintain an adequate system of records and accounts following recognized accounting principles and controls;</p>
          <p>(4) appoint any employees, both permanent and temporary, that are necessary to carry out the provisions of this chapter;</p>
          <p>(5) organize the work of the system as the director deems necessary to fulfill the functions of the system, and define the duties of its employees and delegate to them any powers or duties, subject to the control of the director and under conditions the director may prescribe. Appointments to exercise delegated power must be by written order and shall be filed with the secretary of state;</p>
          <p>(6) with the advice and consent of the board, contract for the services of an approved actuary, professional management services, and any other consulting services as necessary and fix the compensation for those services. The contracts are not subject to competitive bidding under chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/16C">16C</a>. Any approved actuary retained by the executive director shall function as the actuarial advisor of the board and the executive director, and may perform actuarial valuations and experience studies to supplement those performed by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>. Any supplemental actuarial valuations or experience studies shall be filed with the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement. Professional management services may not be contracted for more often than once in six years. Copies of professional management survey reports must be transmitted to the secretary of the senate, the chief clerk of the house of representatives, and the Legislative Reference Library as provided by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/3.195">3.195</a>, and to the executive director of the commission at the time as reports are furnished to the board. Only management firms experienced in conducting management surveys of federal, state, or local public retirement systems are qualified to contract with the director;</p>
          <p>(7) with the advice and consent of the board provide in-service training for the employees of the system;</p>
          <p>(8) make refunds of accumulated contributions to former state employees and to the designated beneficiary, surviving spouse, legal representative, or next of kin of deceased state employees or deceased former state employees, as provided in this chapter;</p>
          <p>(9) determine the amount of the annuities and disability benefits of employees covered by the system and authorize payment of the annuities and benefits beginning as of the dates on which the annuities and benefits begin to accrue, in accordance with the provisions of this chapter;</p>
          <p>(10) pay annuities, refunds, survivor benefits, salaries, and necessary operating expenses of the system;</p>
          <p>(11) certify funds available for investment to the State Board of Investment;</p>
          <p>(12) with the advice and approval of the board request the State Board of Investment to sell securities when the director determines that funds are needed for the system;</p>
          <p>(13) prepare and submit to the board and the legislature an annual financial report covering the operation of the system, as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20">356.20</a>;</p>
          <p>(14) prepare and submit biennial and annual budgets to the board and with the approval of the board submit the budgets to the Department of Management and Budget; and</p>
          <p>(15) with the approval of the board, perform other duties required to administer the retirement and other provisions of this chapter and to do its business.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Directors' fiduciary obligation.</span></h2>
          <p>The board and the director shall undertake their activities consistent with chapter 356A.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Medical adviser.</span></h2>
          <p>The executive director may contract with an accredited independent organization specializing in disability determinations, licensed physicians, or physicians on the staff of the commissioner of health as designated by the commissioner, to be the medical adviser to the system.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Duties of the medical adviser.</span></h2>
          <p>The medical adviser shall designate licensed physicians to examine applicants for disability benefits. The medical adviser shall pass upon medical reports based upon examinations required to determine whether a state employee is totally and permanently disabled as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17">352.01, subdivision 17</a>, shall investigate health and medical statements and certificates by or on behalf of a state employee in connection with a disability benefit, and shall report in writing to the director conclusions and recommendations on matters referred for advice.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Power to determine employee's status.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as otherwise specifically provided in this chapter, the final power to determine the status of any individual in the employ of the state for the purposes of this chapter is vested in the board and its decision is final.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Legal adviser, attorney general.</span></h2>
          <p>The attorney general shall be the legal adviser of the board and of the director. The board may sue or be sued or petitioned under this section in the name of the board of directors of the system. In actions brought by it or against it, the board shall be represented by the attorney general and, except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.96#stat.356.96.13">356.96, subdivision 13</a>, venue of actions shall be in the Ramsey County District Court.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Department of Management and Budget, duties.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon request of the director, the Department of Management and Budget shall furnish information about the status of state employees as required by the director or the board in the performance of their duties.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.1.17.0">1989 c 319 art 1 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.14">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.14">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Departmental information.</span></h2>
          <p>Under the direction of the director the head of each department shall give information and keep records the director or the board needs for their duties.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.15">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 15." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.15">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Calendar year basis optional.</span></h2>
          <p>For all purposes except quarterly and biennial budgets the system may operate on a calendar rather than a fiscal year basis.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.03.16">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 16." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.03.16">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Data processing services.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding chapter 16B or 16C or any law to the contrary, the executive director of the system may use the services of the Department of Administration, Information Services Division, for electronic data processing and related services or may contract for all or a part of the services.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.031"><b>352.031</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.6.3.0">2007 c 134 art 6 s 3</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.04">
        <h1 class="shn">352.04 STATE EMPLOYEES RETIREMENT FUND, CONTRIBUTIONS BY EMPLOYEE AND EMPLOYER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Fund created.</span></h2>
          <p> There is created a special fund to be known as the general state employees retirement fund. In that fund, employee contributions, employer contributions, and other amounts authorized by law must be deposited.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Employee contributions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The employee contribution to the fund must be equal to the following percent of salary:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:7.692307692307693%">
            <col style="width:62.4615384615385%">
            <col style="width:29.8461538461538%">
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2014, to June 30, 2018</td>
              <td>5.5</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2018, to June 30, 2019</td>
              <td>5.75</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2019, to June 30, 2023</td>
              <td>6</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2023, to June 30, 2025</td>
              <td>5.5</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>after June 30, 2025</td>
              <td>6</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(b) These contributions must be made by deduction from salary as provided in subdivision 4.</p>
          <p>(c) Contribution increases under paragraph (a) must be paid starting the first day of the first full pay period after the effective date of the increase.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Employer contributions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The employer contribution to the fund must be equal to the following percent of salary:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:7.692307692307693%">
            <col style="width:53.8461538461538%">
            <col style="width:38.4615384615385%">
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2014, to June 30, 2018</td>
              <td>5.5</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2018, to June 30, 2019</td>
              <td>5.875</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>after June 30, 2019</td>
              <td>6.25</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(b) Contribution increases under paragraph (a) must be paid starting the first day of the first full pay period after the effective date of the increase.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Payroll deductions.</span></h2>
          <p> Each employing entity shall have employee contributions deducted from the salary of each employee covered by the system on every payroll abstract and shall approve one voucher payable to the commissioner of management and budget for the aggregate amount deducted on the payroll abstract. Deductions from salaries of employees paid directly by any employing entity must be made by the officer or employee authorized by law to pay the salaries. Any employing entity having authority to appoint any employee who receives fees as compensation or who receives compensation on federal payrolls shall collect as the required employee contribution the applicable amounts required in subdivision 2. Deductions from salary and amounts collected must be remitted to the director with a statement showing the amount of earnings or fees, and in the case of fees, the number of transactions, the amount of each of the deductions and collections, and the names of the employees on whose account they have been made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Payment of employer contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>The head of each department or agency shall have employer contributions made to the fund on a payroll abstract at the time an employee is paid salary in the amounts required by subdivision 3. These contributions must be charged as administrative costs. Each department shall pay these amounts from accounts and funds from which the department or agency receives its revenue, including appropriations from the general fund and from any other fund for the payment of salaries and in the same proportion as it pays the amounts of salaries.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Quasi-state agencies; employer contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>For those of their employees who are covered by the system, the State Horticultural Society, the Disabled American Veterans, Department of Minnesota, Veterans of Foreign Wars, Department of Minnesota, the Minnesota Crop Improvement Association, the Minnesota Historical Society, the Armory Building Commission, the Minnesota Safety Council, the Metropolitan Council and any of its statutory boards, the employer of persons described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2a">352.01, subdivision 2a</a>, paragraph (a), clause (15), and any other agency employing employees covered by this system, respectively, shall also pay into the retirement fund the amount required by subdivision 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/221/#laws.0.11.0">1973 c 221 s 11</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Employing entity required to pay omitted salary deductions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If an employing entity fails to take deductions past due for a period of 60 days or less from an employee's salary as provided in this section, those deductions must be taken on later payroll abstracts.</p>
          <p>(b) If an employing entity fails to take deductions past due for a period in excess of 60 days from an employee's salary as provided in this section, the employing entity, and not the employee, must pay on later payroll abstracts the employee and employer contributions and interest at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.2">356.59, subdivision 2</a>, compounded annually, from the date the employee and employer contributions should have been deducted to the date payment of the total amount due is paid by the employing entity.</p>
          <p>(c) If an employing entity fails to take deductions past due for a period of 60 days or less and the employee is no longer in state service so that the required deductions cannot be taken from the salary of the employee, the employing entity must nevertheless pay the required employer contributions. If any employing entity fails to take deductions past due for a period in excess of 60 days and the employee is no longer in state service, the omitted contributions must be recovered under paragraph (b).</p>
          <p>(d) If an employee from whose salary required deductions were past due for a period of 60 days or less leaves state service before the payment of the omitted deductions and subsequently returns to state service, the unpaid amount is considered the equivalent of a refund. The employee accrues no right by reason of the unpaid amount, except that the employee may pay the amount of omitted deductions as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.23">352.23</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Erroneous deductions, canceled payments.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Deductions taken from the salary of an employee for the retirement fund in excess of required amounts must, upon discovery and verification by the employing entity making the deduction, be refunded to the employee. Employer contributions made in excess of required amounts must be refunded or credited to the employing entity that made the contribution.</p>
          <p>(b) If a deduction for the retirement fund is taken from a salary payment, and the payment is canceled or the amount of the payment returned to the funds of the employing entity making the payment, the sum deducted, or the part of it required to adjust the deductions, must be refunded or credited to the employing entity if the employing entity applies for the refund on a form furnished by the director.</p>
          <p>(c) If erroneous employee deductions and employer contributions are caused by an error in plan coverage involving the plan and any other plans specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.637">356.637</a>, that section applies. If the employee should have been covered by the plan governed by chapter <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352D">352D</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/353D">353D</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354B">354B</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/354D">354D</a>, the employee deductions and employer contributions taken in error must be directly transferred to the applicable employee's account in the correct retirement plan, with interest at the applicable monthly rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.2">356.59, subdivision 2</a>, compounded annually, from the first day of the month following the month in which coverage should have commenced in the correct defined contribution plan until the end of the month in which the transfer occurs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/2/1/#laws.0.8.0">2Sp1981 c 1 s 8</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.</h2>
          <p>MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.17.3.0">2018 c 211 art 17 s 3</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Fund disbursement restricted.</span></h2>
          <p>The general state employees retirement fund must be disbursed only for the purposes provided by law. The expenses of the system and any benefits provided by law must be paid from the general state employees retirement fund. The retirement allowances, retirement annuities, and disability benefits, as well as refunds of any sum remaining to the credit of a deceased retired employee or a disabled employee must be paid from the general state employees retirement fund. The amounts necessary to make the payments from the general state employees retirement fund are annually appropriated from that fund for those purposes.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.04.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.04.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">"Employing entity" defined.</span></h2>
          <p>In this section, "employing entity" means the entity that pays a state employee's salary and remits retirement contributions.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
