      <div class="section" id="stat.352.041">
        <h1 class="shn">352.041 LEAVE OF ABSENCE FOR EMPLOYMENT BY POLITICAL SUBDIVISION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.041.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.041.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Allowable service credit.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An employee covered by the general state employees retirement plan who is given a leave of absence for employment by a political subdivision of the state remains a member of the plan and must continue to pay member contributions into the general state employees retirement fund for the period of leave.</p>
          <p>(b) Upon payment of member contributions, the employee must be given allowable service credit as a state employee on the records of the retirement plan as though the employee had received salary from the state during the leave. Payments into the retirement fund must be at the rate required in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04#stat.352.04.2">352.04, subdivision 2</a>, and must be based upon the salary received from the political subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.041.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.041.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Employee contributions, procedure.</span></h2>
          <p>The officer or employee who is authorized by law to pay salaries to employees of the political subdivision which is employing a state employee must deduct employee contributions for the general state employees retirement plan under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04#stat.352.04.2">352.04, subdivision 2</a>, from the salary of each employee who is on leave of absence from state service on each payroll abstract and must pay the sum to the director following the conclusion of each pay period.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.041.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.041.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Employer contributions, procedure.</span></h2>
          <p>The officer or employee who is authorized by law to pay salaries to employees of the political subdivision which is employing a state employee covered by the general state employees retirement plan also must have employer contributions made to the general state employees retirement fund following the conclusion of each payroll abstract in the amount required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04#stat.352.04.3">352.04, subdivision 3</a>. These contributions must be charged to the political subdivision as an administrative cost.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.041.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.041.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.041.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.041.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Employer contributions, leaves of absence; tax levies.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Every political subdivision which is employing a state employee covered by the system on leave of absence from state service for employment by a political subdivision of the state must pay into the general state employees retirement fund the amount of the employer contribution required by law for state employees covered by the system under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04#stat.352.04.3">352.04, subdivision 3</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) Employing political subdivisions other than school districts may levy taxes necessary for the payment of employer contributions without limitation as to rate or amount. The levy of the taxes does not reduce the amount of other taxes that may be levied by political subdivisions other than school districts which are subject to any limitation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.041.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.041.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.36.0">1983 c 128 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.041.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.041.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, 1963 c 383 s 59]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.041.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/0/647/#laws.0.1.0">1959 c 647 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.20.0">1963 c 383 s 20</a>,21; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/57/#laws.0.12.0">Ex1967 c 57 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/1/31/#laws.20.10.0">Ex1971 c 31 art 20 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.17.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 17</a>-20</p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.045"><b>352.045</b> MS 2016 [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.1.11.0">2018 c 211 art 1 s 11</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.05">
        <h1 class="shn">352.05 COMMISSIONER OF MANAGEMENT AND BUDGET TO BE TREASURER OF SYSTEM.</h1>
        <p>The commissioner of management and budget is ex officio treasurer of the retirement funds of the system. The general bond to the state shall cover all liability for actions as treasurer of these funds. Funds of the system received by the commissioner of management and budget must be set aside in the state treasury to the credit of the proper fund. The commissioner of management and budget shall deliver to the director copies of all payroll abstracts of the state together with the commissioner of management and budget's payments covering the deductions made on these payroll abstracts for the retirement fund. The director shall have a list made of the commissioner of management and budget's payments. These payments must then be credited to the retirement fund. The commissioner of management and budget shall pay out of this fund only upon abstracts signed by the director, or by the finance officer designated by the director during the disability or the absence of the director from the city of St. Paul, Minnesota. Abstracts for investments may be signed by the executive director of the State Board of Investment.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.05.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/5">254-5</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1929/0/191/#laws.0.5.0">1929 c 191 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1931/0/351/#laws.0.5.0">1931 c 351 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1935/0/238/#laws.0.5.0">1935 c 238 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1941/0/391/#laws.0.5.0">1941 c 391 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/284/#laws.0.8.0">1945 c 284 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.28.0">1957 c 928 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/1/67/#laws.0.7.0">Ex1961 c 67 s 7</a>; Ex1967 c 57 c 13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/492/#laws.0.14.0">1973 c 492 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/112/#laws.2.39.0">2003 c 112 art 2 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/10/#laws.3.40.0">1Sp2019 c 10 art 3 s 40</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.06"><b>352.06</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/404/#laws.0.8.0">1967 c 404 s 8</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.061">
        <h1 class="shn">352.061 INVESTMENT BOARD TO INVEST FUNDS.</h1>
        <p>The director shall, from time to time, certify to the State Board of Investment any portions of the state employees retirement fund that in the judgment of the director are not required for immediate use. The State Board of Investment shall invest and reinvest sums so certified in securities that are duly authorized legal investments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/11A.24">11A.24</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.061.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/0/404/#laws.0.7.0">1967 c 404 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/57/#laws.0.14.0">Ex1967 c 57 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/893/#laws.0.6.0">1969 c 893 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.14.45.0">1980 c 607 art 14 s 45</a> subd 2; s 46; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.12.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 12</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.07"><b>352.07</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.08"><b>352.08</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.09"><b>352.09</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.33.0">1957 c 928 s 33</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.10"><b>352.10</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1945/0/284/#laws.0.1.0">1945 c 284 s 1</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr_by_subd" id="stat.352.103">
        <h1>352.103 </h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.103.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.103.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.103.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.103.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/230/#laws.0.18.0">1965 c 230 s 18</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.11"><b>352.11</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.33.0">1957 c 928 s 33</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.113">
        <h1 class="shn">352.113 PERMANENT DISABILITY BENEFITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Age and service requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An employee covered by the system, who has satisfied the applicable allowable service credit requirement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.1">352.115, subdivision 1</a>, has not reached normal retirement age, and becomes totally and permanently disabled is entitled to a disability benefit in an amount provided in subdivision 3.</p>
          <p>(b) If the disabled employee's state service has terminated at any time, the employee must have at least two years of allowable service after last becoming a state employee covered by the system.</p>
          <p>(c) Refunds may be repaid under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.23">352.23</a> before the effective accrual date of the disability benefit under subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application; accrual of benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An employee making claim for a total and permanent disability benefit, or someone acting on behalf of the employee upon proof of authority satisfactory to the director, shall file a written application for benefits in an office of the system or with a person authorized by the director.</p>
          <p>(b) The application must be in a form and manner prescribed by the director. The completed application form and supporting documents must be received in an office of the system or by an authorized person before the expiration of the period specified in subdivision 4, paragraph (g). In this paragraph, "supporting documents" means:</p>
          <p>(1) two medical reports as required by subdivision 4, paragraph (b); and</p>
          <p>(2) a written certification by the employing entity as required by subdivision 4, paragraph (e).</p>
          <p class="bl">Supporting documents are not required to be original documents except as determined by the director.</p>
          <p>(c) The benefit shall begin to accrue the day following the start of disability or the day following the last day paid, whichever is later, but not earlier than 180 days before the date the application and supporting documents are filed in an office of the system or with an authorized person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Computation of benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>The total and permanent disability benefit must be computed in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a>. The disability benefit shall be the normal annuity without reduction for each month the employee is under normal retirement age at the time of becoming disabled. A disabled employee may choose to receive the normal disability benefit or an optional annuity as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.3">352.116, subdivision 3</a>. This choice must be made before the start of payment of the disability benefit and is effective the date on which the disability begins to accrue as provided in subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Medical or psychological examinations; authorization for payment of benefit.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Any physician, psychologist, chiropractor, physician assistant, podiatrist, or APRN providing any service specified in this section must be licensed.</p>
          <p>(b) An applicant shall provide a detailed report signed by a physician, and at least one additional report signed by a physician, psychologist, chiropractor, physician assistant, podiatrist, or an APRN with evidence to support an application for total and permanent disability. The reports must include an expert opinion regarding whether the employee is permanently and totally disabled within the meaning of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17">352.01, subdivision 17</a>, and that the disability arose before the employee was placed on any paid or unpaid leave of absence or terminated public service.</p>
          <p>(c) If there is medical evidence that supports the expectation that at some point the person applying for the disability benefit will no longer be disabled, the decision granting the disability benefit may provide for a termination date upon which the total and permanent disability can be expected to no longer exist. When a termination date is part of the decision granting benefits, prior to the benefit termination the executive director shall review any evidence provided by the disabled employee to show that the disabling condition for which benefits were initially granted continues. If the benefits cease, the disabled employee may follow the appeal procedures described in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.96">356.96</a> or may reapply for disability benefits using the process described in this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(d) Any claim to disability must be supported by a report from the employer indicating that there is no available work that the employee can perform with the disabling condition and that all reasonable accommodations have been considered. Upon request of the executive director, an employer shall provide evidence of the steps the employer has taken to attempt to provide reasonable accommodations and continued employment to the claimant.</p>
          <p>(e) The director shall also obtain written certification from the employer stating whether the employment has ceased or whether the employee is on sick leave of absence because of a disability that will prevent further service to the employer and that the employee is not entitled to compensation from the employer.</p>
          <p>(f) The medical adviser shall consider the reports of the physician, psychologist, chiropractor, physician assistant, podiatrist, or APRN and any other evidence supplied by the employee or other interested parties. If the medical adviser finds the employee totally and permanently disabled, the adviser shall make appropriate recommendation to the director in writing together with the date from which the employee has been totally disabled. The director shall then determine if the disability occurred while still in the employment of the state and constitutes a total and permanent disability as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17">352.01, subdivision 17</a>.</p>
          <p>(g) A terminated employee may apply for a disability benefit within 18 months of termination as long as the disability occurred while in the employment of the state. The fact that an employee is placed on leave of absence without compensation because of disability does not bar that employee from receiving a disability benefit.</p>
          <p>(h) Upon appeal, the board of directors may extend the disability benefit application deadline in paragraph (g) by an additional 18 months if the terminated employee is determined by the board of directors to have a cognitive impairment that made it unlikely that the terminated employee understood that there was an application deadline or that the terminated employee was able to meet the application deadline.</p>
          <p>(i) Unless the payment of a disability benefit has terminated because the employee is no longer totally disabled, or because the employee has reached normal retirement age as provided in this section, the disability benefit must cease with the last payment received by the disabled employee or which had accrued during the lifetime of the employee unless there is a spouse surviving. In that event, the surviving spouse is entitled to the disability benefit for the calendar month in which the disabled employee died.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Independent medical examination or vocational rehabilitation counseling.</span></h2>
          <p>Any individual applying for or receiving disability benefits shall submit to an independent medical examination or an assessment by a certified rehabilitation counselor if requested by the executive director or designee. The examination must be paid for by the system.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/7/#laws.0.36.0">1Sp1985 c 7 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Regular medical or psychological examinations.</span></h2>
          <p>At least once each year during the first five years following the allowance of a disability benefit to any employee, and at least once in every three-year period thereafter, the director may require any disabled employee to provide medical, chiropractic, or psychological evidence to support the continuation of the total and permanent disability. The evidence must be in a form and manner prescribed by the executive director for review by an expert or experts designated by the medical adviser and engaged by the director. If the medical information provided to the medical adviser indicates that the employee is no longer permanently and totally disabled, or is engaged in or can engage in a gainful occupation, payments of the disability benefit by the fund must be discontinued. The payments must be discontinued as soon as the employee is reinstated to the payroll following a sick leave of absence, but in no case may payment be made for more than 60 days after the medical adviser finds that the employee is no longer permanently and totally disabled.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Partial reemployment.</span></h2>
          <p>If the disabled employee resumes a gainful occupation from which earnings are less than the employee's salary at the date of disability or the salary currently paid for similar positions, the director shall continue the disability benefit in an amount which when added to earnings does not exceed the salary at the date of disability, provided the disability benefit in this case does not exceed the disability benefit originally allowed. Deductions for the retirement fund must not be taken from the salary of a disabled employee who is receiving a disability benefit as provided in this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.7a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.7a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Temporary reemployment benefit reduction waiver.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A reduction in benefits under subdivision 7, or a termination of benefits due to the disabled employee resuming a gainful occupation from which earnings are equal to or more than the employee's salary at the date of disability or the salary currently paid for similar positions does not apply until six months after the individual returns to a gainful occupation.</p>
          <p>(b) No deductions for the retirement fund may be taken from the salary of a disabled person who is attempting to return to work under this provision unless the member waives further disability benefits.</p>
          <p>(c) A member may return to employment and continue disability benefit payments under this subdivision only once while receiving disability benefits from a retirement plan administered by the Minnesota State Retirement System.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Refusal of examination.</span></h2>
          <p>If a person applying for a disability benefit refuses to submit to a medical or psychological examination, the disability application shall be rejected. If a disability benefit recipient refuses to submit to a medical or psychological examination as required, payments by the fund must be discontinued and the director shall revoke all rights of the employee in any disability benefit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Return to state service.</span></h2>
          <p>Any employee receiving a disability benefit who is restored to active state service except employees receiving benefits as provided in subdivision 7, shall have deductions taken for the retirement fund and upon retirement have the payable retirement annuity based upon all allowable service including that upon which the disability benefits were based. No employee is entitled to receive disability benefits and a retirement annuity at the same time.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Employee again disabled after resuming employment.</span></h2>
          <p>If a disabled employee resumes gainful employment with the state and is not entitled to continued payment of a disability benefit as provided in subdivision 7, the right to a disability benefit ends when the employee has been employed for one year thereafter. If the employee again becomes totally and permanently disabled before reaching normal retirement age, application for a disability benefit may again be made. If the employee is entitled to a disability benefit it must be computed as provided in subdivision 9.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Recomputation of benefit.</span></h2>
          <p>If an employee who has resumed employment as provided in subdivision 10 is reemployed for more than three months, but cannot continue for one year, the disability benefit must be recomputed allowing additional service credit for the period of reemployment. If the period of reemployment does not exceed three months, the deductions taken from salary after resuming employment must be returned to the employee. The employee is not entitled to service credit for the period covered by the returned deductions.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Retirement status at normal retirement age.</span></h2>
          <p>The disability benefit paid to a disabled employee under this section ends when the employee reaches normal retirement age. If the disabled employee is still totally and permanently disabled when the employee reaches normal retirement age, the employee shall be considered to be a retired employee. If the employee had chosen an optional annuity under subdivision 3, the employee shall receive an annuity in accordance with the terms of the optional annuity previously chosen. If the employee had not chosen an optional annuity pursuant to subdivision 3, the employee may then choose to receive either a normal retirement annuity equal in amount to the disability benefit paid before the employee reached normal retirement age or an optional annuity as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.3">352.116, subdivision 3</a>. The choice of an optional annuity must be made before reaching normal retirement age. If an optional annuity is chosen, the choice is effective on the date the employee attains normal retirement age and the optional annuity shall begin to accrue the first of the month following the month in which the employee attains this age.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A disability benefit under this section is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.113.14">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.113.14">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Disabilitant earnings reports.</span></h2>
          <p>Disability benefit recipients must report all earnings from reemployment and income from workers' compensation to the system annually by May 15 in a format prescribed by the executive director. The executive director may waive the earnings report requirement for any disabled employee who is not required to undergo regular medical or psychological examinations under subdivision 6. If the form is not submitted by June 15, benefits must be suspended effective July 1. If the form deemed acceptable by the executive director is received after the June 15 deadline, benefits shall be reinstated retroactive to July 1.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.113.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1951/0/441/#laws.0.21.0">1951 c 441 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1955/0/239/#laws.0.15.0">1955 c 239 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.13.0">1957 c 928 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.3.2.0">2006 c 271 art 3 s 2</a>,42; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.13.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 13</a>; art 2 s 9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.2.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.2.13.0">2013 c 111 art 2 s 13</a>-17; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.9.7.0">2018 c 211 art 9 s 7</a>,8; art 15 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.1.4.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.5.6.0">2020 c 108 art 5 s 6</a>,7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/65/#laws.5.2.0">2022 c 65 art 5 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.5.3.0">2024 c 102 art 5 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.115">
        <h1 class="shn">352.115 RETIREMENT ANNUITY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Age and service requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>After separation from state service, any employee who has attained the age of at least 55 years is entitled, upon application, to a retirement annuity if the employee:</p>
          <p>(1) has received credit for at least three years of allowable service and was employed before July 1, 2010;</p>
          <p>(2) has received credit for at least five years of allowable service if employed after June 30, 2010, and terminated employment before July 1, 2023;</p>
          <p>(3) was actively employed on July 1, 2023, and has earned three years of allowable service prior to the employee's retirement application;</p>
          <p>(4) has three or more years of allowable service if employed after June 30, 2023; or</p>
          <p>(5) has received credit for at least 30 years of allowable service regardless of age.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Normal retirement annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>The retirement annuity hereunder payable at normal retirement age or thereafter must be computed in accordance with the applicable provisions of the formula stated in subdivision 3, on the basis of the employee's average salary for the period of allowable service. This retirement annuity is known as the "normal" retirement annuity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Retirement annuity formula.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) This paragraph, in conjunction with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.1">352.116, subdivision 1</a>, applies to a person who became a covered employee or a member of a pension fund listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, before July 1, 1989, unless paragraph (b), in conjunction with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.1a">352.116, subdivision 1a</a>, produces a higher annuity amount, in which case paragraph (b) applies. The employee's average salary, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.14a">352.01, subdivision 14a</a>, multiplied by 1.2 percent per year of allowable service for the first ten years and 1.7 percent for each later year of allowable service and pro rata for completed months less than a full year determines the amount of the retirement annuity to which the employee is entitled.</p>
          <p>(b) This paragraph applies to a person who has become at least 55 years old and first became a covered employee after June 30, 1989, and to any other covered employee who has become at least 55 years old and whose annuity amount, when calculated under this paragraph and in conjunction with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.1a">352.116, subdivision 1a</a>, is higher than it is when calculated under paragraph (a), in conjunction with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.1">352.116, subdivision 1</a>. The employee's average salary, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.14a">352.01, subdivision 14a</a>, multiplied by 1.7 percent for each year of allowable service and pro rata for months less than a full year determines the amount of the retirement annuity to which the employee is entitled.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.36.0">1983 c 128 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.36.0">1983 c 128 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/230/#laws.0.18.0">1965 c 230 s 18</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Application for annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>Application for annuity or optional annuity payment may be made by the employee at time of retirement, or by someone acting on behalf of the employee upon proof of authority satisfactory to the director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.7a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.7a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Application procedure.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The filing of an application for an annuity, refund, disability benefit, survivor benefit, death benefit, or other monthly benefit authorized by this chapter or chapter 3A, 352B, 352D, or 490 must comply with this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(b) Filing of an application under paragraph (a) is not complete until an original application and supporting documents are received in an office of the system or received by a person authorized by the director. An original application may not be an electronic copy or facsimile copy and if received in an office of the system, must be delivered by personal service or mail.</p>
          <p>(c) In this subdivision, "supporting documents" are:</p>
          <p>(1) documents sufficient to verify birth;</p>
          <p>(2) documents sufficient to verify marital status or establish the terms of a divorce, if applicable; and</p>
          <p>(3) the acknowledgment required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.46#stat.356.46.3">356.46, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (b).</p>
          <p class="bl">Supporting documents are not required to be original documents except as determined by the director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Accrual of annuity.</span></h2>
          <p> The application for an annuity must not be made more than 60 days before the time the state employee or former state employee elects to begin collecting a retirement annuity. If the director determines an applicant for annuity has fulfilled the legal requirements for an annuity, the director shall authorize the annuity payment in accordance with this chapter and payment must be made as authorized. An annuity shall begin to accrue no earlier than 180 days before the date the application is filed with the director, but not before the day following the termination of state service or before the day the employee is eligible to retire by reason of both age and service requirements. The retirement annuity shall cease with the last payment which had accrued during the lifetime of the retired employee unless an optional annuity provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.3">352.116, subdivision 3</a>, had been selected and had become payable. The joint and last survivor annuity shall cease with the last payment received by the survivor during the lifetime of the survivor. If a retired employee had not selected an optional annuity, or a survivor annuity is not payable under the option, and a spouse survives, the spouse is entitled only to the annuity for the calendar month in which the retired employee died. If an optional annuity is payable after the death of the retired employee, the survivor is entitled to the annuity for the calendar month in which the retired employee died.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Annuities payable monthly.</span></h2>
          <p>All annuities, and disability benefits authorized by this chapter, must be paid in equal monthly installments and must not be increased, decreased, or revoked except as provided in this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Reemployment of annuitant.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except for salary or wages received as a temporary employee of the legislature during a legislative session, if any retired employee again becomes entitled to receive salary or wages from any employer who employs state employees as that term is defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2">352.01, subdivision 2</a>, in a position covered by this chapter, the annuity or retirement allowance must cease the first of the month following the month that the retired employee has earned an amount equal to the annual maximum earnings allowable for that age for the continued receipt of full benefit amounts monthly under the federal old age, survivors, and disability insurance program as set by the secretary of health and human services under United States Code, title 42, section 403, in any calendar year. If the retired employee has not yet reached the minimum age for the receipt of Social Security benefits, the maximum earnings for the retired employee are equal to the annual maximum earnings allowable for the minimum age for the receipt of Social Security benefits.</p>
          <p>(b) The balance of the annual retirement annuity after cessation must be handled or disposed of as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.47">356.47</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) The annuity must be resumed the first of the month following the month that state service ends, or, if the retired employee is still employed at the beginning of the next calendar year, at the beginning of that calendar year, and payment must again end when the retired employee has earned the applicable reemployment earnings maximum specified in this subdivision. If the retired employee is granted a sick leave without pay, but not otherwise, the annuity or retirement allowance must be resumed during the period of sick leave.</p>
          <p>(d) No payroll deductions for the retirement fund may be made from the earnings of a reemployed retired employee.</p>
          <p>(e) No change may be made in the monthly amount of an annuity or retirement allowance because of the reemployment of an annuitant.</p>
          <p>(f) If a reemployed annuitant whose annuity is suspended under paragraph (a) is having insurance premium amounts withheld under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.87#stat.356.87.2">356.87, subdivision 2</a>, insurance premium amounts must continue to be withheld and transferred from the suspended portion of the annuity. The balance of the annual retirement annuity after cessation, after deduction of the insurance premium amounts, must be treated as specified in paragraph (b).</p>
          <p>(g) If a reemployed annuitant whose annuity is suspended under paragraph (a) has a former spouse receiving a portion of the annuity allowable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.58">518.58</a>, subdivision 1, the portion payable to the former spouse must continue to be paid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Accrued annuity at death.</span></h2>
          <p>Any annuity accrued before the death of a retired employee, and any disability benefit accrued before the death of a disabled employee shall be paid to the beneficiary whom the retired employee or the disabled employee had last designated. If (1) no beneficiary has been so designated, or (2) the designated beneficiary dies before making claim for payment of an annuity or benefit, payment must be made to the surviving spouse, or, if none, to the employee's surviving children in equal shares or, if none, to the employee's surviving parents in equal shares or, if none, to the legal representative of the retired employee or disabled employee. If the designated beneficiary, surviving spouse, or legal representative entitled to the annuity does not apply for payment within five years from the date of death of the retired employee or disabled employee, the annuity or disability benefit which had accrued at the time of death must be credited to and become a part of the retirement fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Death, return of payments.</span></h2>
          <p>If at the time of death a retired employee, a disabled employee, or a survivor has in possession commissioner of management and budget's payments covering a retirement annuity, disability benefit or survivor benefit from the retirement fund, in the absence of probate proceedings, and upon the return of the payments for cancellation, payment of the accrued annuity or benefit, shall be made as provided in subdivision 11, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.12#stat.352.12.4">352.12, subdivision 4</a>. Payments made under this subdivision shall be a bar to recovery by any other person or persons.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.276.0">1981 c 224 s 276</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.115.14">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 14." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.115.14">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A retirement annuity under this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116">352.116</a> is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.115.history
			">

      <div class="section" id="stat.352.1155">
        <h1 class="shn">352.1155 NO ANNUITY REDUCTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.1155.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.1155.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as indicated in subdivision 4, the annuity reduction provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.10">352.115, subdivision 10</a>, do not apply to a person who:</p>
          <p>(1) retires from the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system with at least ten years of combined service credit in a system under the jurisdiction of the Board of Trustees of the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities;</p>
          <p>(2) was employed on a full-time basis immediately preceding retirement as a faculty member or as an unclassified administrator in that system;</p>
          <p>(3) was not a recipient of an early retirement incentive under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/136F.481">136F.481</a>;</p>
          <p>(4) begins drawing an annuity from the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System; and</p>
          <p>(5) returns to work on not less than a one-third time basis and not more than a two-thirds time basis in the system from which the person retired under an agreement in which the person may not earn a salary of more than $62,000 in a calendar year through employment after retirement in the system from which the person retired.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.1155.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.1155.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Approval requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>Initial participation, the amount of time worked, and the duration of participation under this section must be mutually agreed upon by the president of the institution where the person returns to work and the employee. The president may require up to one-year notice of intent to participate in the program as a condition of participation under this section. The president shall determine the time of year the employee shall work. The employer or the president may not require a person to waive any rights under a collective bargaining agreement as a condition of participation under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.1155.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.1155.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Service credit prohibition.</span></h2>
          <p>Notwithstanding any law to the contrary, a person eligible under this section may not, based on employment to which the waiver in this section applies, earn further service credit in a Minnesota public defined benefit plan and is not eligible to participate in a Minnesota public defined contribution plan, other than a firefighter relief association governed by chapter 424A or the statewide volunteer firefighter plan governed by chapter 353G. No employer or employee contribution to any of these plans may be made on behalf of such a person.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.1155.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.1155.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Exemption limit.</span></h2>
          <p>For a person eligible under this section who earns more than $62,000 in a calendar year through reemployment in the Minnesota State Colleges and Universities system following retirement, the annuity reduction provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.10">352.115, subdivision 10</a>, apply only to income over $62,000.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.1155.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.1155.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Continuing rights.</span></h2>
          <p>A person who returns to work under this section is a member of the appropriate bargaining unit and is covered by the appropriate collective bargaining contract. Except as provided in this section, the person's coverage is subject to any part of the contract limiting rights of part-time employees.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.1155.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.19.3.0">1999 c 222 art 19 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.2.3.0">2000 c 461 art 2 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.8.2.0">2014 c 296 art 8 s 2</a>,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.3.1.0">2024 c 102 art 3 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.116">
        <h1 class="shn">352.116 ANNUITIES UPON RETIREMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.116.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.116.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Reduced annuity before normal retirement age.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) This subdivision applies only to a person who first became a covered employee or a member of a pension fund listed in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30#stat.356.30.3">356.30, subdivision 3</a>, before July 1, 1989, and whose annuity is higher when calculated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.3">352.115, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (a), in conjunction with this subdivision than when calculated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.3">352.115, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (b), in conjunction with subdivision 1a.</p>
          <p>(b) Any employee who is eligible for a retirement annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.1">352.115, subdivision 1</a>, and who retires before normal retirement age with credit for at least three but less than 30 years of allowable service shall be paid the normal retirement annuity provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3, paragraph (a), reduced by one-quarter of one percent for each month that the employee is under normal retirement age at the time of retirement. An employee who is eligible for a retirement annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.1">352.115, subdivision 1</a>, and who retires prior to age 62 with credit for at least 30 years of allowable service shall be paid the normal retirement annuity provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3, paragraph (a), reduced by one-quarter of one percent for each month that the employee is under age 62 at the time of retirement.</p>
          <p>(c) Any person whose attained age plus credited allowable service totals 90 years is entitled, upon application, to a retirement annuity in an amount equal to the normal annuity provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3, paragraph (a), without any reduction by reason of early retirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.116.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.116.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Actuarial reduction for early retirement.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) This subdivision applies to a person who has become at least 55 years old and first became a covered employee after June 30, 1989, and to any other covered employee who has become at least 55 years old and whose annuity is higher when calculated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.3">352.115, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (b), in conjunction with this subdivision than when calculated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.3">352.115, subdivision 3</a>, paragraph (a), in conjunction with subdivision 1. A covered employee who retires before the normal retirement age shall be paid the normal retirement annuity provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3, paragraph (b), reduced as described in paragraph (b) or (c), as applicable.</p>
          <p>(b) For covered employees who retire on or after July 1, 2019, the reduced annuity is the actuarial equivalent of the annuity that would be payable to the employee if the employee deferred receipt of the annuity until normal retirement age and the annuity amount were augmented at the applicable annual rate, compounded annually, from the day the annuity begins to accrue until the normal retirement age. The applicable annual rate is the rate in effect on the employee's effective date of retirement and shall be considered as fixed for the employee for the period until the employee reaches normal retirement age. The applicable annual rates are the following:</p>
          <p>(1) until June 30, 2019, three percent if the employee became an employee before July 1, 2006, and 2.5 percent if the employee became an employee after June 30, 2006;</p>
          <p>(2) beginning July 1, 2019, through June 30, 2024, a rate that changes each month, on the first day of the month, starting with the rate in clause (1), as applicable to the employee, and reducing the rate to zero in equal monthly increments over the five-year period; and</p>
          <p>(3) after June 30, 2024, zero percent.</p>
          <p>After June 30, 2024, actuarial equivalent, for the purpose of determining the reduced annuity commencing before normal retirement age under this clause, shall not take into account any augmentation.</p>
          <p>(c) For covered employees who retire before July 1, 2019, the reduced annuity is the actuarial equivalent of the annuity that would be payable to the employee if the employee deferred receipt of the annuity until normal retirement age and the annuity amount were augmented at an annual rate of three percent, compounded annually, from the day the annuity begins to accrue until normal retirement age if the employee became an employee before July 1, 2006, and at an annual rate of 2.5 percent, compounded annually, from the day the annuity begins to accrue until normal retirement age if the employee became an employee after June 30, 2006.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.116.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.116.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Normal annuity at normal retirement age.</span></h2>
          <p>Any employee who retires after reaching normal retirement age shall be paid the annuity provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.116.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.116.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Optional annuities.</span></h2>
          <p>The board shall establish an optional retirement annuity in the form of a joint and survivor annuity. The board may also establish an optional annuity in the form of an annuity payable for a period certain and for life thereafter. In addition, the board may also establish an optional annuity that takes the form of an annuity calculated on the basis of the age of the retired employee at retirement and payable for the period before the retired employee becomes eligible for Social Security old age retirement benefits in a greater amount than the amount of the annuity calculated under subdivision 2 on the basis of the age of the retired employee at retirement. For each year that the retiring employee is under age 62, up to five percent of the total single life annuity required reserves may be used to accelerate the optional retirement annuity. This greater amount shall be paid until the end of the month in which the retired employee reaches age 62, at which time the annuity shall be reduced. Except as provided in subdivision 3a, the optional forms must be actuarially equivalent to the normal single life annuity forms provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116">352.116</a>, whichever applies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.116.3a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.116.3a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Bounce-back annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a retired employee or disabilitant selects a joint and survivor annuity option under subdivision 3 after June 30, 1989, the retired employee or disabilitant must receive a normal single-life annuity if the designated optional annuity beneficiary dies before the retired employee or disabilitant. Under this option, no reduction may be made in the annuity to provide for restoration of the normal single-life annuity in the event of the death of the designated optional annuity beneficiary.</p>
          <p>(b) The annuity adjustment specified in paragraph (a) also applies to joint and survivor annuity options under subdivision 3 elected before July 1, 1989. The annuity adjustment under this paragraph occurs on July 1, 1989, or on the first day of the first month following the death of the designated optional annuity beneficiary, whichever is later. This paragraph may not be interpreted as authorizing retroactive payments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.116.3b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.116.3b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Bounce-back annuity; correctional employees and State Patrol.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The board of directors must provide a joint and survivor annuity option to members of the correctional employees and State Patrol retirement funds. Under this option, if a former member or disabilitant selects a joint and survivor annuity option after June 30, 1989, the former member or disabilitant must receive a normal single life annuity if the designated optional annuity beneficiary dies before the former member or disabilitant. Under this option, no reduction may be made in the person's annuity to provide for restoration of the normal single life annuity in the event of the death of the designated optional annuity beneficiary.</p>
          <p>(b) The annuity adjustment specified in paragraph (a) also applies to joint and survivor annuity options elected before July 1, 1989. The annuity adjustment under this paragraph occurs on July 1, 1989, or on the first day of the first month following the death of the designated optional annuity beneficiary, whichever is later. This paragraph may not be interpreted as authorizing retroactive payments.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.116.3c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.116.3c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3c.<span class="headnote">Effective date of bounce-back annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>In the event of the death of the designated optional annuity beneficiary before the retired employee or disabilitant, the restoration of the normal single life annuity under subdivision 3a or 3b will take effect as of the first of the month following the date of death of the designated optional annuity beneficiary or on the first of the month following one year before the date on which a certified copy of the death record of the designated optional annuity beneficiary is received in the Office of the Minnesota State Retirement System, whichever date is later.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.116.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.116.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Determining actuarial equivalency.</span></h2>
          <p>In establishing the procedure for determining the actuarial equivalency of early retirement annuities as required under subdivision 1a or in establishing actuarial equivalent optional retirement annuity forms as required under subdivision 3, the board shall obtain the written recommendation of the actuary retained under section 356.214. The recommendations shall be a part of the permanent records of the board.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.116.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.10.0">1957 c 928 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/6/#laws.0.11.0">Ex1959 c 6 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1961/1/67/#laws.0.11.0">Ex1961 c 67 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.33.0">1963 c 383 s 33</a>-35; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/57/#laws.0.17.0">Ex1967 c 57 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/37/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 37 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/274/#laws.0.1.0">1971 c 274 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/653/#laws.0.31.0">1973 c 653 s 31</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/781/#laws.0.1.0">1978 c 781 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/509/#laws.0.130.0">1980 c 509 s 130</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.49.0">1981 c 224 s 49</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; art 11 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/259/#laws.0.14.0">1987 c 259 s 14</a>-16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.1.6.0">1989 c 319 art 1 s 6</a>; art 13 s 10; art 17 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.7.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 7</a>-9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/464/#laws.1.39.0">1992 c 464 art 1 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/9/#laws.15.32.0">1Sp2001 c 9 art 15 s 32</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.3.3.0">2006 c 271 art 3 s 3</a>,4,47; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/277/#laws.2.1.0">2006 c 277 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.1.2.0">2018 c 211 art 1 s 2</a></p>
      </div>

          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.119.2">§</a>
