      <div class="section" id="stat.352.12">
        <h1 class="shn">352.12 REFUND AFTER DEATH.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Death before termination of service.</span></h2>
          <p>If an employee dies before state service has terminated and neither a survivor annuity nor a bounce-back annuity is payable on behalf of the employee, or if a former employee who has sufficient service credit to be entitled to an annuity dies before the annuity has become payable, a refund in an amount equal to the employee's accumulated contributions plus interest is payable upon filing a written application on a form prescribed by the executive director. The refund is payable to the last designated beneficiary or, if there is none, to the surviving spouse or, if none, to the employee's surviving children in equal shares or, if none, to the employee's surviving parents in equal shares or, if none, to the representative of the estate. Interest must be computed as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.22#stat.352.22.2">352.22, subdivision 2</a>. Upon the death of an employee who has received a refund that was later repaid in full, interest must be paid on the repaid refund only from the date of the repayment. If the repayment was made in installments, interest must be paid only from the date on which the installment payments began. The designated beneficiary, the surviving spouse, or the representative of the estate of an employee who had received a disability benefit is not entitled to the payment of interest upon any balance remaining to the decedent's credit in the fund at the time of death, unless the death occurred before any payment could be negotiated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Surviving spouse benefit.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If an employee or former employee has satisfied the applicable allowable service credit requirement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a>, subdivision 1, and dies before an annuity or disability benefit has become payable, notwithstanding any designation of beneficiary to the contrary, the surviving spouse of the employee may elect to receive, in lieu of the refund with interest under subdivision 1, an annuity equal to the joint and 100 percent survivor annuity which the employee or former employee could have qualified for on the date of death.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the employee was an active employee at the time of the employee's death, was under age 55, and has credit for at least 30 years of allowable service on the date of death, the surviving spouse may elect to receive a 100 percent joint and survivor annuity based on the age of the employee and surviving spouse on the date of death. The annuity is payable using the full early retirement reduction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.1">352.116, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (b), to age 55 and one-half of the early retirement reduction from age 55 to the age payment begins.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) If the employee was an active employee at the time of the employee's death, was under age 55, and has satisfied the applicable allowable service credit requirement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.1">352.115, subdivision 1</a>, but did not yet qualify for retirement, the surviving spouse may elect to receive a 100 percent joint and survivor annuity based on the age of the employee and surviving spouse at the time of death. The annuity is payable using the full early retirement reduction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.1">352.116, subdivision 1</a> or 1a, to age 55 and one-half of the early retirement reduction from age 55 to the age payment begins.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The surviving spouse eligible for benefits under paragraph (a) may apply for the annuity at any time after the date on which the employee or former employee would have attained the required age for retirement based on the allowable service earned. The surviving spouse eligible for surviving spouse benefits under paragraph (b) or (c) may apply for the annuity at any time after the employee's death. The annuity must be computed under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115, subdivisions 1, 2, and 3</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116">352.116, subdivisions 1</a>, 1a, and 3. Section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.22">352.22, subdivisions 3</a> and 3a, apply to a deferred annuity payable to a surviving spouse under this subdivision. The annuity must cease with the last payment received by the surviving spouse in the lifetime of the surviving spouse, or upon expiration of a term certain benefit payment to a surviving spouse under subdivision 2a. An amount equal to the excess, if any, of the accumulated contributions credited to the account of the deceased employee in excess of the total of the benefits paid and payable to the surviving spouse must be paid to the deceased employee's or former employee's last designated beneficiary or, if none, as specified under subdivision 1.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) Any employee or former employee may request in writing, with the signed consent of the spouse, that this subdivision not apply and that payment be made only to a designated beneficiary as otherwise provided by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Surviving spouse coverage term certain.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) In lieu of the 100 percent optional annuity under subdivision 2, or refund under subdivision 1, the surviving spouse of a deceased employee or former employee may elect to receive survivor coverage in a term certain of ten, 15, or 20 years. The monthly term certain annuity must be actuarially equivalent to the 100 percent optional annuity under subdivision 2.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If a survivor elects a term certain annuity and dies before the expiration of the specified term certain period, the commuted value of the remaining annuity payments must be paid in a lump sum to the survivor's estate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.2b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.2b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Dependent child survivor coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>If there is no surviving spouse eligible for benefits under subdivision 2, a dependent child or children as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.26">352.01, subdivision 26</a>, is eligible for monthly payments under this subdivision, but only if the dependent child or children did not elect to receive a refund under subdivision 1. Payments to a dependent child must be paid from the date of the employee's death to the date the dependent child attains age 20 if the child is under age 15. If the child is 15 years or older on the date of death, payment must be made for five years. The payment to a dependent child is an amount actuarially equivalent to the value of a 100 percent optional annuity under subdivision 2 using the age of the employee and age of the dependent child at the date of death in lieu of the age of the surviving spouse. If there is more than one dependent child, each dependent child shall receive a proportionate share of the actuarial value of the employee's account.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.2c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.2c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2c.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A survivor benefit under subdivision 2, 2a, or 2b is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Refund of $3,000 or less.</span></h2>
          <p>If a state employee or former state employee dies without having designated a beneficiary, or if the beneficiary should die before applying for refund of the sum to the credit of the deceased employee or former employee, and there is no surviving spouse, and the amount of the refund does not exceed $3,000 exclusive of interest, the director may refund the amount to the deceased or former employee's next of kin. The amount may be refunded 90 days after the date of death of the employee or former employee in the absence of probate proceedings, and upon proper application. The next of kin must be determined by the director with the concurrence of the board, to be entitled to the refund consistent with the laws of descent. A determination and payment without notice are conclusive and final and are a bar against claims of all other persons.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Refund to minor beneficiary.</span></h2>
          <p>If an employee or former employee dies having named as a beneficiary a person who is a minor at the time of the application for refund, and the amount of the refund does not exceed $3,000, exclusive of interest, the director in the absence of guardianship or probate proceedings may make payment to the natural guardian having custody of the minor beneficiary, for the benefit of the child. Any annuity, retirement allowance, or disability benefit accrued at the time of death of a disabled or retired employee, payable to a minor beneficiary, may similarly be paid. Payment is a bar to recovery by any other person or persons.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.7.1.0">1993 c 307 art 7 s 1</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Death after service termination.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in subdivision 1, if a former employee covered by the system who has not received an annuity, a retirement allowance, or a disability benefit dies, a refund is payable to the last designated beneficiary or, if there is none, to the surviving spouse or, if none, to the employee's surviving children in equal shares or, if none, to the employee's surviving parents in equal shares or, if none, to the representative of the estate in an amount equal to accumulated employee contributions plus interest. The interest on the refund must be computed as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.22#stat.352.22.2">352.22, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Absence of optional or bounce-back annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon the death of a retired employee who selected neither an optional annuity or a bounce-back annuity, a refund must be paid in an amount equal to the excess, if any, of the accumulated contributions to the credit of the retired employee immediately before retirement in excess of the sum of (1) all annuities, retirement allowances, and disability benefits that had been received and had accrued in the lifetime of the decedent, and (2) the annuity, retirement allowance, or disability benefit if not negotiated, payable to the surviving spouse under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.8">352.115, subdivision 8</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113#stat.352.113.4">352.113, subdivision 4</a>, for the calendar month in which the retired employee died. The refund must be paid to the named beneficiary or, if there be none, to the surviving spouse or, if none, to the employee's surviving children in equal shares or, if none, to the employee's surviving parents in equal shares or, if none, to the representative of the estate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Optional or bounce-back annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>If the last eligible recipient of an optional annuity dies and the total amounts paid under it are less than the accumulated contributions to the credit of the retired employee immediately before retirement, the balance of accumulated contributions must be paid to the person designated by the retired employee in writing to receive payment. If no designation has been made by the retired employee, the remaining balance of accumulated contributions must be paid to the surviving children of the deceased recipient of the optional annuity in equal shares. If there are no surviving children, payment must be made to the deceased recipient's parents or, if none, to the representative of the deceased recipient's estate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Beneficiary designation.</span></h2>
          <p>The designation of a beneficiary or person to receive any accumulated contributions remaining to the credit of an employee, a former employee, or a retired employee, at the time of death, as provided in this section, must be in writing and must be filed with the director before the death of the employee, former employee, or retired employee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Death of beneficiary before refund.</span></h2>
          <p>If the last designated beneficiary or beneficiaries and the surviving spouse of a (1) deceased employee, (2) former employee, or (3) retired employee, dies before receiving a refund of the sum to the credit of the deceased employee, former employee, or retired employee at the time of death, the refund must be made to the estate of the deceased employee or as provided in subdivision 3 if the amount of the refund does not exceed $3,000 exclusive of interest.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Death of disability annuitant.</span></h2>
          <p>If an employee who has received a disability benefit dies, a payment must be made of an amount equal to the excess, if any, of the accumulated contributions to the credit of the employee at the time the disability benefit began to accrue over and above the aggregate of (1) all disability benefits received and which had accrued during life, and (2) the benefit for the month in which the disabled employee died, payable, if applicable, to the surviving spouse under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113#stat.352.113.4">352.113, subdivision 4</a>. The payment must be paid to the last designated beneficiary or, if there be none, to the surviving spouse, or if none, to the employee's surviving children in equal shares or, if none, to the employee's surviving parents in equal shares or, if none, to the representative of the estate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.12">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 12." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.12">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Refund, failure to request.</span></h2>
          <p>If the last designated beneficiary, surviving spouse, legal representative, or next of kin, as determined by the director with the concurrence of the board, fails to claim the refund as provided in this section (1) within five years from the date of death of a retired employee or disabled employee, or (2) within five years after the last deduction was taken from the salary of a deceased employee or deceased former employee, the accumulated contributions of the deceased employee, former employee, retired employee, or disabled employee must be credited to the retirement fund. However, if claim to refund is made within ten years after the transfer of accumulated contributions to the fund or within ten years after the date of death, whichever is later, and the amount transferred to the fund is over $25, the sum must be restored to the account of the deceased employee, former employee, retired employee, or disabled employee. The refund must then be made to the surviving spouse or, if none, to the legal representative of the estate irrespective of any designation of beneficiary made by the deceased employee, former employee, retired employee, or disabled employee.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.12.13">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 13." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.12.13">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Refund, beneficiary.</span></h2>
          <p>If upon death a former employee has in possession a commissioner of management and budget's payment which does not exceed $1,000 covering a refund of accumulated contributions in the retirement fund, in the absence of probate proceedings the commissioner of management and budget's payment may be returned for cancellation, and then upon application made by the last designated beneficiary of the deceased former employee, refund of the accumulated contributions must be paid to the last designated beneficiary. Payments made under this subdivision are a bar to recovery by any other person or persons.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.12.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/5">254-11</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1929/0/191/#laws.0.14.0">1929 c 191 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/326/#laws.0.1.0">1933 c 326 s 1</a>;       <div class="sr" id="stat.352.13"><b>352.13</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.14"><b>352.14</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.15">
        <h1 class="shn">352.15 EXEMPTION FROM PROCESS AND TAXATION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.15.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.15.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Exemption.</span></h2>
          <p>The provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.401">356.401</a> apply to the general state employees retirement plan and to the correctional state employees retirement plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.15.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.15.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.81.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 81</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.15.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.15.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.1.1.0">1994 c 528 art 1 s 1</a>5]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.15.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.15.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Deducting health or dental insurance premiums.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may authorize, at its discretion, the deduction of a retiree's health or dental insurance premiums and transfer of the amounts to a health or dental insurance carrier covering state employees. The insurance carrier must certify that the retired employee has signed an authorization for the deduction and provide a computer readable roster of covered retirees and amounts. The health or dental insurance carrier must refund deductions withheld from a retiree's check in error directly to the retiree. The board shall require that the insurance carrier reimburse the fund for the administrative expense of withholding the premium amounts. The insurance carrier shall assume liability for any failure of the system to properly withhold the premium amounts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.15.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.15.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Direct transfer of refunds.</span></h2>
          <p>A direct transfer of refunds under this chapter may be made to an individual retirement savings account or a qualified retirement plan of the person upon the receipt of an application for transfer by a former employee, on forms acceptable to the executive director.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.15.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/5">254-17</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1929/0/191/#laws.0.17.0">1929 c 191 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1931/0/351/#laws.0.0.0">1931 c 351</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/326/#laws.0.0.0">1933 c 326</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1935/0/238/#laws.0.15.0">1935 c 238 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1941/0/391/#laws.0.14.0">1941 c 391 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/6/#laws.0.21.0">Ex1959 c 6 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1971/0/789/#laws.0.4.0">1971 c 789 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/368/#laws.0.30.0">1975 c 368 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1979/0/303/#laws.3.28.0">1979 c 303 art 3 s 28</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.12.0">1983 c 128 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/547/#laws.0.4.0">1984 c 547 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/157/#laws.0.2.0">1987 c 157 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.1.18.0">1993 c 307 art 1 s 18</a>; art 3 s 3,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/254/#laws.1.89.0">1995 c 254 art 1 s 89</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/203/#laws.6.92.0">1997 c 203 art 6 s 92</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.3.1.0">2000 c 461 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2003/0/127/#laws.3.18.0">2003 c 127 art 3 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.10.21.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 10 s 21</a>-23</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.16">
        <h1 class="shn">352.16 INSURANCE LAWS NOT TO APPLY.</h1>
        <p>None of the laws of this state regulating insurance or insurance companies apply to the Minnesota State Retirement System or any of its funds.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.16.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">(<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/1940A/cite/5">254-18</a>) <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1929/0/191/#laws.0.18.0">1929 c 191 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1933/0/326/#laws.0.0.0">1933 c 326</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1935/0/238/#laws.0.16.0">1935 c 238 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1941/0/391/#laws.0.15.0">1941 c 391 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/57/#laws.0.19.0">Ex1967 c 57 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.17"><b>352.17</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.18"><b>352.18</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.22">
        <h1 class="shn">352.22 REFUND OR DEFERRED ANNUITIES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Service termination.</span></h2>
          <p>Any employee who ceases to be a state employee by reason of termination of state service or layoff is entitled to a refund provided in subdivision 2 or a deferred retirement annuity as provided in subdivision 3. Application for a refund may be made after the termination of state service or layoff if the applicant has not again become a state employee required to be covered by the system.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Amount of refund.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as provided in subdivision 3, the refund payable to a person who ceased to be a state employee by reason of a termination of state service is an amount equal to employee accumulated contributions plus interest until the date on which the refund is paid, at the following rates for the applicable period:</p>
          <p>(1) six percent per year compounded daily from the date that the contribution was made until June 30, 2011;</p>
          <p>(2) four percent per year compounded daily from the date that the contribution was made or July 1, 2011, whichever is later, until June 30, 2018; and</p>
          <p>(3) three percent per year compounded daily from the date that the contribution was made or July 1, 2018, whichever is later.</p>
          <p>Included with the refund is any interest paid as part of repayment of a past refund, plus interest thereon from the date of repayment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Certain refund repayments prohibited.</span></h2>
          <p>No refunds of contributions made to the metropolitan transit commission-transit operating division employees retirement fund received before July 1, 1978, or for service rendered before July 1, 1978, may be repaid.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.2b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.2b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2b.<span class="headnote">Refund repayment.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person who has received a refund from the state employees retirement plan, and who is a member of any of the retirement plans specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.311">356.311</a>, paragraph (b), may repay the refund with interest to the state employees retirement plan. If a refund is repaid to the plan and more than one refund has been received from the plan, all refunds must be repaid. Repayment must be made as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.23">352.23</a>, and under terms and conditions consistent with that section as agreed upon with the director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Deferred annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An employee who has at least three years of allowable service if employed before July 1, 2010, or who has at least five years of allowable service if employed after June 30, 2010, when termination occurs may elect to leave the accumulated contributions in the fund and thereby be entitled to a deferred retirement annuity. The annuity must be computed under the law in effect when state service terminated, on the basis of the allowable service credited to the person before the termination of service.</p>
          <p>(b) An employee on layoff or on leave of absence without pay, except a leave of absence for health reasons, and who does not return to state service must have an annuity, deferred annuity, or other benefit to which the employee may become entitled computed under the law in effect on the employee's last working day.</p>
          <p>(c) No application for a deferred annuity may be made more than 60 days before the time the former employee reaches the required age for entitlement to the payment of the annuity. The deferred annuity begins to accrue no earlier than 60 days before the date the application is filed in the office of the system, but not (1) before the date on which the employee reaches the required age for entitlement to the annuity nor (2) before the day following the termination of state service in a position which is not covered by the retirement system.</p>
          <p>(d) Application for the accumulated contributions left on deposit with the fund may be made at any time following the date of the termination of service.</p>
          <p>(e) Deferred annuities must be augmented as provided in subdivision 3a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.3a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.3a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Computation of deferred annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The deferred annuity of any former state employee must be augmented from the first day of the month following termination of active service or July 1, 1971, whichever is later, to the effective date of retirement.</p>
          <p>(b) For a person who became a state employee before July 1, 2006, the annuity must be augmented at the following rate or rates, compounded annually:</p>
          <p>(1) five percent until January 1, 1981;</p>
          <p>(2) three percent thereafter until January 1 of the year following the year in which the former employee attains age 55 or January 1, 2012, whichever is earlier;</p>
          <p>(3) five percent from the January 1 next following the attainment of age 55 until December 31, 2011;</p>
          <p>(4) two percent from January 1, 2012, until December 31, 2018; and</p>
          <p>(5) after December 31, 2018, the deferred annuity must not be augmented.</p>
          <p>(c) For a person who became a state employee after June 30, 2006, the annuity must be augmented at the following rate or rates, compounded annually:</p>
          <p>(1) 2.5 percent until December 31, 2011;</p>
          <p>(2) two percent from January 1, 2012, until December 31, 2018; and</p>
          <p>(3) after December 31, 2018, the deferred annuity must not be augmented.</p>
          <p>(d) The retirement annuity or disability benefit of, or the survivor benefit payable on behalf of, a former state employee who terminated service before July 1, 1997, which is not first payable until after June 30, 1997, must be increased on an actuarial equivalent basis to reflect the change in the investment return actuarial assumption under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215#stat.356.215.8">356.215, subdivision 8</a>, from five percent to six percent under a calculation procedure and the tables adopted by the board and approved by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.3b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.3b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Segmented annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a person otherwise entitled to an annuity has more than one period of uninterrupted service, the person is entitled to augmentation under subdivision 3a for each period of uninterrupted service. "Uninterrupted service" for the purpose of this subdivision means periods of covered employment during which the employee has not been separated from state service for more than two years. The formula percentages used for each period of uninterrupted service must be those applicable to a new employee. The mortality and investment rate of return assumptions used to compute the annuity must be those in effect when the employee files an application for annuity.</p>
          <p>(b) If a person repays a refund, the service restored by the repayment must be considered continuous with the next period of service for which the employee has credit with this system.</p>
          <p>(c) The requirements and provisions for retirement before normal retirement age in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.1">352.115, subdivision 1</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116">352.116</a> also apply to an employee fulfilling the requirements with a combination of service as provided in this subdivision.</p>
          <p>(d) This subdivision does not reduce the annuity otherwise payable under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.3c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.3c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3c.<span class="headnote">Adjustment for certain deferred annuities.</span></h2>
          <p>The retirement annuity or disability benefit of, or the survivor benefit payable on behalf of, a former state employee who terminated service before July 1, 1997, which is not first payable until after June 30, 1997, must be increased on an actuarial equivalent basis to reflect the change in the postretirement interest rate actuarial assumption under Laws 1997, chapter 233, section 58, from five percent to six percent under a calculation procedure and the tables adopted by the board and approved by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.36.0">1983 c 128 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Refund generally unlimited.</span></h2>
          <p>The right of refund provided in this section is not restricted as to time unless specifically provided and the statute of limitation does not apply to it.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/230/#laws.0.18.0">1965 c 230 s 18</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, Ex1967 c 57 so 20]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Refund specifically limited.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If a former employee covered by the system does not apply for refund within five years after the last deduction was taken from salary for the retirement fund, and does not have enough service to qualify for a deferred annuity, accumulated member and employer contributions must be credited to and become a part of the retirement fund.</p>
          <p>(b) If the former employee returns to state service and becomes a state employee covered by the system, the amount credited to the retirement fund, if more than $25, must be restored to the individual account. If the amount credited to the fund is over $25 and the former employee applies for refund or an annuity under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.30">356.30</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.311">356.311</a>, the amount must be restored to the former employee's individual account and a refund made or an annuity paid, whichever applies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.7.1.0">1993 c 307 art 7 s 1</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.10">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 10." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.10">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Other refunds.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Former employees covered by the system are entitled to apply for refunds if they are or become members of the State Patrol retirement fund, the state Teachers Retirement Association, or employees of the University of Minnesota excluded from coverage under the system by action of the Board of Regents; or employees of the adjutant general who under federal law effectually elect membership in a federal retirement system; or officers or employees of the senate or house of representatives, excluded from coverage under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2b">352.01, subdivision 2b</a>, clause (6), item (i). The refunds must include accumulated contributions plus interest as provided in subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.22.11">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 11." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.22.11">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/342/#laws.0.21.0">1980 c 342 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.352.23">
        <h1 class="shn">352.23 TERMINATION OF RIGHTS; REPAYMENT OF REFUND.</h1>
        <p>(a) When any employee accepts a refund as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.22">352.22</a>, all existing allowable service credits and all rights and benefits to which the employee was entitled before accepting the refund terminate.</p>
        <p>(b) Terminated service credits and rights must not again be restored until the former employee acquires at least six months of allowable service credit after taking the last refund and repays all refunds previously taken from the retirement fund with interest as provided in paragraph (d). An employee will not be considered as entitled to any other benefit, including benefits for which the employee may be eligible because of the employee's original hire date into public employment, until full repayment of all refunds has been made.</p>
        <p>(c) Repayment of refunds entitles the employee only to credit for service covered by (1) salary deductions; (2) payments previously made in lieu of salary deductions as permitted under law in effect when the payment in lieu of deductions was made; (3) payments made to obtain credit for service as permitted by laws in effect when payment was made; and (4) allowable service previously credited while receiving temporary workers' compensation as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.11">352.01, subdivision 11</a>, paragraph (a), clause (3).</p>
        <p>(d) Payments under this section for repayment of refunds are to be paid with interest at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.2">356.59, subdivision 2</a>, compounded annually, from the date the refund was taken until the date the refund is repaid. Repayment may be made in a lump sum during employment or up to six months after termination from service.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.23.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.15.0">1957 c 928 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/6/#laws.0.22.0">Ex1959 c 6 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/230/#laws.0.14.0">1965 c 230 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/57/#laws.0.21.0">Ex1967 c 57 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/1083/#laws.0.1.0">1969 c 1083 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/653/#laws.0.37.0">1973 c 653 s 37</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.14.34.0">1980 c 607 art 14 s 34</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/598/#laws.1.4.0">1992 c 598 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.2.5.0">1993 c 307 art 2 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.2.5.0">2015 c 68 art 2 s 5</a>; art 13 s 9,64; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.6.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 6</a>; art 9 s 9; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.1.7.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 1 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.5.8.0">2020 c 108 art 5 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.24"><b>352.24</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.241"><b>352.241</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.25"><b>352.25</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.26"><b>352.26</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.59.0">1963 c 383 s 59</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.27">
        <h1 class="shn">352.27 FEDERALLY PROTECTED PURCHASE OF SERVICE CREDIT FOR PERIODS OF MILITARY SERVICE.</h1>
        <p class="in">(a) An employee who is absent from employment by reason of service in the uniformed services, as defined in United States Code, title 38, section 4303(13), and who returns to state service upon discharge from service in the uniformed service within the time frames required in United States Code, title 38, section 4312(e), may obtain service credit for the period of the uniformed service as further specified in this section, provided that the employee did not separate from uniformed service with a dishonorable or bad conduct discharge or under other than honorable conditions.</p>
        <p class="in">(b) The employee may obtain credit by paying into the fund an equivalent employee contribution based upon the contribution rate or rates in effect at the time that the uniformed service was performed multiplied by the full and fractional years being purchased and applied to the annual salary rate. The annual salary rate is the average annual salary during the purchase period that the employee would have received if the employee had continued to be employed in covered employment rather than having performed uniformed service, or, if the determination of that rate is not reasonably certain, the annual salary rate is the employee's average salary rate during the 12-month period of covered employment rendered immediately preceding the period of the uniformed service.</p>
        <p class="in">(c) The equivalent employer contribution and, if applicable, the equivalent additional employer contribution provided in this chapter must be paid by the department employing the employee from funds available to the department at the time and in the manner provided in this chapter, using the employer and additional employer contribution rate or rates in effect at the time that the uniformed service was performed, applied to the same annual salary rate or rates used to compute the equivalent employee contribution.</p>
        <p class="in">(d) If the employee equivalent contributions provided in this section are not paid in full, the employee's allowable service credit must be prorated by multiplying the full and fractional number of years of uniformed service eligible for purchase by the ratio obtained by dividing the total employee contribution received by the total employee contribution otherwise required under this section.</p>
        <p class="in">(e) To receive service credit under this section, the equivalent employee contributions specified in paragraph (b) must be transmitted to the Minnesota State Retirement System during the period which begins with the date on which the individual returns to state service and which has a duration of three times the length of the uniformed service period, but not to exceed five years. If the determined payment period is less than three years, the contributions required under paragraph (b) to receive service credit must be made within three years of the discharge date.</p>
        <p class="in">(f) The amount of service credit obtainable under this section may not exceed five years unless a longer purchase period is required under United States Code, title 38, section 4312.</p>
        <p class="in">(g) The employing unit shall pay interest on all equivalent employee and employer contribution amounts payable under this section. Interest must be at the applicable annual rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.2">356.59, subdivision 2</a>, compounded annually, from the end of each fiscal year of the leave or the break in service to the end of the month in which the payment is received.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.27.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1957/0/928/#laws.0.20.0">1957 c 928 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1959/1/6/#laws.0.25.0">Ex1959 c 6 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1963/0/383/#laws.0.53.0">1963 c 383 s 53</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1965/0/230/#laws.0.15.0">1965 c 230 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1967/1/57/#laws.0.22.0">Ex1967 c 57 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1969/0/188/#laws.0.3.0">1969 c 188 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/653/#laws.0.38.0">1973 c 653 s 38</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/598/#laws.1.5.0">1992 c 598 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.3.1.0">2004 c 267 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.7.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.3.2.0">2015 c 68 art 3 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.7.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/65/#laws.1.1.0">2022 c 65 art 1 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.271"><b>352.271</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.65.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 65</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.272">
        <h1 class="shn">352.272 STATE-AUTHORIZED PURCHASE OF SERVICE CREDIT FOR PERIODS OF MILITARY SERVICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.272.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.272.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Service credit purchase authorized.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Unless prohibited under paragraph (b), an employee is eligible to purchase service credit, not to exceed five cumulative years of service credit, for one or more periods of service in the uniformed services, as defined in United States Code, title 38, section 4303(13), if:</p>
          <p>(1) the employee has at least three years of service credit with the general state employees retirement plan or the correctional state employees retirement plan under this chapter;</p>
          <p>(2) the duration of the employee's current period of employment is at least six months; and</p>
          <p>(3) one of the following applies:</p>
          <p>(i) the employee's service in the uniformed services occurred before becoming a state employee as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2">352.01, subdivision 2</a>; or</p>
          <p>(ii) the employee did not obtain service credit for a period of military service under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.27">352.27</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A service credit purchase is prohibited if:</p>
          <p>(1) the employee separated from uniformed service with a dishonorable or bad conduct discharge or under other than honorable conditions; or</p>
          <p>(2) the employee has purchased or otherwise received service credit from any Minnesota public employee pension plan for the same period of service in the uniformed services.</p>
          <p>(c) When purchasing a period of service, if the period of service in the uniformed services is one year or less, then the employee must purchase the full period of service. If the period of service in the uniformed services is longer than one year, the employee may purchase the full period, not to exceed five cumulative years, or may purchase a portion of the period of service. If the employee purchases a portion of the period of service in the uniformed services, the portion must:</p>
          <p>(1) not be less than one year; and</p>
          <p>(2) be in increments of six months of service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.272.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.272.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Application and documentation.</span></h2>
          <p>To purchase service credit under subdivision 1, an employee must apply to the executive director to make the purchase. The application must include all necessary documentation of the employee's qualifications to make the purchase, signed written permission to allow the executive director to request and receive necessary verification of applicable facts and eligibility requirements, and any other relevant information that the executive director may require. The employee must submit with the application payment of the administrative fee in the amount of $250 to cover the costs of calculating the purchase payment amount under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.551">356.551</a>. If the employee proceeds with the purchase, the administrative fee will be credited toward the purchase payment amount.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.272.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.272.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Purchase payment amount; service credit grant.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The purchase payment amount is the amount determined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.551">356.551</a> for the period or periods of service requested, except that, for purposes of calculating the purchase payment amount to purchase service credit for service in the uniformed services that occurred before becoming a state employee or between periods of employment as a state employee, section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.551">356.551</a>, subdivision 2, paragraph (c), does not apply.</p>
          <p>(b) Service credit must be granted by the applicable plan to the purchasing employee upon the executive director's receipt of the purchase payment amount. The service credit purchased under this section may not be used for the purpose of determining a disability benefit under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113">352.113</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.95">352.95</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) Payment must be made before the effective date of the employee's retirement.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

        </p>
      </div>

      <div class="section" id="stat.352.75">
        <h1 class="shn">352.75 TRANSFER OF PENSION COVERAGE SAVINGS CLAUSE; INCREASE IN EXISTING ANNUITIES AND BENEFITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.75.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.75.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.65.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 65</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.75.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.75.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">New employees.</span></h2>
          <p>All persons employed by the Metropolitan Council as employees of the Transit Operating Division are members of the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System and are state employees for purposes of this chapter unless specifically excluded under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.2b">352.01, subdivision 2b</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.75.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.75.3">§</a>
