      <div class="history" id="stat.352.75.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1978/0/538/#laws.0.8.0">1978 c 538 s 8</a>-10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.14.35.0">1980 c 607 art 14 s 35</a>,45 subd 2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.56.0">1981 c 224 s 56</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/628/#laws.3.29.0">1994 c 628 art 3 s 29</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/305/#laws.1.75.0">1996 c 305 art 1 s 75</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.16.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 16</a>,17; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.9.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.10.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 10</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.76"><b>352.76</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.65.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 65</a>]</div>
      <h1 class="fgh">SPECIAL COVERAGE</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.85">
        <h1 class="shn">352.85 MILITARY AFFAIRS PERSONNEL.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.85.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.85.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility; retirement annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>Any person who is employed by the Department of Military Affairs other than as a full-time firefighter, who is covered by the general employee retirement plan of the system as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.23">352.01, subdivision 23</a>, who is ordered to active duty under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/190.08#stat.190.08.3">190.08, subdivision 3</a>, who elects this special retirement coverage under subdivision 4, who is required to retire from federal military status at an age earlier than normal retirement age by applicable federal laws or regulations, and who terminates employment as a state employee upon attaining that mandatory retirement age is entitled, upon application, to a retirement annuity computed in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3, without any reduction for early retirement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.1">352.116, subdivision 1</a> or 1a.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.85.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.85.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Disability benefit.</span></h2>
          <p>An employee described in subdivision 1, who is less than the applicable federal military status mandatory retirement age and who becomes disabled and physically or mentally unfit to perform occupational duties due to injury, sickness, or other disability, and who is found disqualified for retention on active duty as a result of a physical examination required by applicable federal laws or regulations, is entitled upon application to disability benefits computed in the manner specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113">352.113</a>. Disability benefits are otherwise governed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113">352.113</a>, except that the age for the termination of the disability benefit is the applicable federal military status mandatory retirement age.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.85.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.85.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Additional contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>The special retirement annuities and disability benefits authorized by this section shall be financed by an employee contribution from the covered Department of Military Affairs employee of 1.6 percent and an employer contribution from the Department of Military Affairs of 1.6 percent. These contributions are in addition to the contributions required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3. They must be made as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04, subdivisions 4, 5, and 6</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.85.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.85.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Election of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>To be covered by this section, any employee of the Department of Military Affairs, described in subdivision 1, must file a notice with the executive director of the system on a form prescribed by the executive director stating that the employee elects to be covered. Notice must be filed within 90 days of employment. Elected coverage is effective retroactively as of the first day of employment. Amounts that would have been deducted from the employee's salary starting with the first day of employment but were not deducted because the employee had not yet filed the election must be deducted from the employee's future salary in accordance with a schedule of deductions determined by the executive director and the Department of Military Affairs. Elections are irrevocable during any period of covered employment. A failure to file a timely notice is deemed a waiver of coverage by this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.85.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.85.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Restriction on coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>Nothing in this section applies to the adjutant general.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.85.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.85.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/269/#laws.3.21.0">1991 c 269 art 3 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.85.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.15.22.0">1980 c 607 art 15 s 22</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.57.0">1981 c 224 s 57</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/575/#laws.0.1.0">1982 c 575 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/7/#laws.0.35.0">1Sp1985 c 7 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/259/#laws.0.18.0">1987 c 259 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/372/#laws.1.3.0">1987 c 372 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1988/0/709/#laws.9.1.0">1988 c 709 art 9 s 1</a>,2; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.13.19.0">1989 c 319 art 13 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.1.19.0">1993 c 307 art 1 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.1.8.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 1 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.86">
        <h1 class="shn">352.86 TRANSPORTATION DEPARTMENT PILOTS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.86.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.86.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to any employee of the Department of Transportation in the civil service employment classification of aircraft pilot or chief pilot, who is covered for that employment by the general employee retirement plan, and who elected before June 1, 2008, special retirement coverage under this section by an irrevocable election on forms provided by the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.86.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.86.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.</h2>
          <p>MS 2008 [Renumbered subd 6]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.86.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.86.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>MS 2008 [Renumbered subd 7]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.86.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.86.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.12.17.0">2009 c 169 art 12 s 17</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.86.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.86.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/269/#laws.3.21.0">1991 c 269 art 3 s 21</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.86.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.86.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Retirement annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>An eligible person under subdivision 1 who terminates employment as a state employee on or after age 62 but prior to normal retirement age is entitled, upon application, to a retirement annuity computed under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115">352.115</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3, without any reduction for early retirement under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.1">352.116, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.86.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.86.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Disability benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>An employee described in subdivision 1, who becomes disabled and physically or mentally unfit to perform occupational duties due to injury, sickness, or other disability, and who is found disqualified for retention as chief pilot or pilot as a result of a physical examination required by applicable federal laws or regulations, may submit an application for disability benefits calculated under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113#stat.352.113.3">352.113, subdivision 3</a>. In considering the disability benefit application, the executive director must use the disability standard specified in this subdivision rather than the total and permanent standard specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113#stat.352.113.1">352.113, subdivision 1</a>. If disability benefits commence under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113">352.113</a>, subdivision 3, the appointing authority shall also provide payments from the state airports fund, totaling 75 percent of current monthly salary less the amount payable under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113#stat.352.113.3">352.113, subdivision 3</a>. Payments from the state airports fund must be made for five years or until normal retirement age, whichever is earlier. These benefits are in lieu of all other state benefits for the disability, including, but not limited to, workers' compensation benefits.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.86.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.86.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Additional contributions.</span></h2>
          <p> An employee covered by this section must pay an additional employee contribution of 1.6 percent of salary. The Department of Transportation must pay an additional employer contribution of 1.6 percent of salary. These contributions are in addition to the contributions required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3. They must be made in the manner provided for in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04, subdivisions 4, 5, and 6</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.86.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/575/#laws.0.3.0">1982 c 575 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/293/#laws.0.98.0">1983 c 293 s 98</a>,99; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/0/248/#laws.0.70.0">1985 c 248 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/7/#laws.0.35.0">1Sp1985 c 7 s 35</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; art 11 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/259/#laws.0.19.0">1987 c 259 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.7.1.0">2004 c 267 art 7 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.12.1.0">2009 c 169 art 12 s 1</a>-3</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.87">
        <h1 class="shn">352.87 STATE FIRE MARSHAL DIVISION EMPLOYEES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.87.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.87.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A member of the general plan who is employed by the Department of Public Safety, State Fire Marshal Division, as a deputy state fire marshal, fire/arson investigator, who elects special benefit coverage under subdivision 8, is entitled to retirement benefits or disability benefits, as applicable, as stated in this section for eligible service under this section rendered after July 1, 1999, for which allowable service credit is received. The covered member must be at least age 55 to qualify for the retirement annuity specified in subdivision 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.87.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.87.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Retirement annuity eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A person specified in subdivision 1 who meets all eligibility requirements specified in this chapter applicable to general plan members is eligible for retirement benefits as specified in subdivision 3.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.87.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.87.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Retirement annuity formula.</span></h2>
          <p>A person specified in subdivision 1 is entitled to receive a retirement annuity applicable for allowable service credit under this section calculated by multiplying the employee's average salary, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.14a">352.01, subdivision 14a</a>, by 2.0 percent for each year or portions of a year of allowable service credit. No reduction for retirement before the normal retirement age, as specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.25">352.01, subdivision 25</a>, applies to service to which this section applies.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.87.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.87.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Non-job-related disability benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>An eligible member described in subdivision 1 who becomes disabled and physically or mentally unfit to perform the duties of the position because of sickness or injury while not engaged in covered employment is entitled to a disability benefit amount equivalent to an annuity computed under subdivision 3 assuming the member has 15 years of service qualifying under this section and waiving the minimum age requirement. If the eligible member becomes disabled under this subdivision with more than 15 years of service covered under this section, the eligible member is entitled to a disability benefit amount equivalent to an annuity computed under subdivision 3 based on all years of service credited under this section and waiving the minimum age requirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.87.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.87.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Job-related disability benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>An eligible member defined in subdivision 1 who becomes disabled and physically or mentally unfit to perform the duties of the position because of sickness or injury while engaged in covered employment is entitled to a disability benefit amount equivalent to an annuity computed under subdivision 3 assuming the member has 20 years of service qualifying under this section and waiving the minimum age requirement. An eligible member who becomes disabled under this subdivision with more than 20 years of service credited under this section is entitled to a disability benefit amount equivalent to an annuity computed under subdivision 3 based on all years of service credited under this section and waiving the age requirement.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.87.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.87.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Disability benefit coordination.</span></h2>
          <p>If the eligible employee is entitled to receive a disability benefit as provided in subdivision 4 or 5 and has allowable service credit under this section for less service than the length of service upon which the disability benefit in subdivision 4 or 5 is based, and also has allowable service in the general plan not includable in this section, the employee is entitled to a disability benefit or deferred retirement annuity based on the general plan service not includable in this section only for the service that, when combined with the service includable in this section, exceeds the number of years on which the disability benefit provided in subdivision 4 or 5 is based. The benefit recipient under subdivision 4 or 5 who also has credit for regular plan service must in all respects qualify under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113">352.113</a> to be entitled to receive a disability benefit based on the general plan service not includable in this section, except that the service may be combined to satisfy length of service requirements. Any deferred annuity to which the employee may be entitled based on general plan service not includable in this section must be augmented as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.22#stat.352.22.3a">352.22, subdivision 3a</a>, while the employee is receiving a disability benefit under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.87.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.87.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Additional contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>The special retirement annuity and disability coverage under this section must be financed by an employee contribution of 2.78 percent of covered salary and an employer contribution of 4.20 percent of covered salary. These contributions are in addition to the contributions required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04</a>, subdivisions 2 and 3, and must be made in the manner provided for in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04, subdivisions 4, 5, and 6</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.87.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.87.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Election of coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>To be covered by this section, an employee of the Department of Public Safety described in subdivision 1 who is employed in a position described in that subdivision must file a notice with the executive director of the system on a form prescribed by the executive director stating that the employee elects to be covered by this section. Notice must be filed within 90 days of employment. Elected coverage is effective retroactively as of the first day of employment. Amounts that would have been deducted from the employee's salary starting with the first day of employment but were not deducted because the employee had not yet filed the election must be deducted from the employee's future salary in accordance with a schedule of deductions determined by the executive director and the Department of Public Safety. Elections are irrevocable during any period of covered employment. A failure to file a timely notice is deemed a waiver of coverage by this section.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.87.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.15.1.0">1999 c 222 art 15 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/10/#laws.3.9.0">1Sp2001 c 10 art 3 s 9</a>,10; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.52.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.1.5.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 1 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.4.2.0">2013 c 111 art 4 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.11.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2019/1/8/#laws.1.9.0">1Sp2019 c 8 art 1 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/65/#laws.9.1.0">2022 c 65 art 9 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">CORRECTIONAL EMPLOYEES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.90">
        <h1 class="shn">352.90 POLICY.</h1>
        <p>It is the policy of the legislature to provide special retirement benefits for and special contributions by certain correctional employees who may be required to retire at an early age because they lose the mental or physical capacity required to maintain the safety, security, discipline, and custody of inmates at state correctional facilities; of patients in the state-operated forensic services program, which is comprised of the Minnesota Security Hospital, the forensic nursing home, the forensic transition service, and the competency restoration program; of patients in the Minnesota Sex Offender Program; or of patients in the Minnesota Specialty Health System-Cambridge.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.90.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/653/#laws.0.39.0">1973 c 653 s 39</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/600/#laws.0.2.0">1980 c 600 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.58.0">1981 c 224 s 58</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/408/#laws.8.9.0">1996 c 408 art 8 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.13.1.0">1999 c 222 art 13 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.2.1.0">2006 c 271 art 2 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/286/#laws.3.1.0">2012 c 286 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.5.1.0">2014 c 296 art 5 s 1</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.91">
        <h1 class="shn">352.91 COVERED CORRECTIONAL SERVICE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Qualifying jobs.</span></h2>
          <p>"Covered correctional service" means service performed by a state employee, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01">352.01</a>, employed at a state correctional facility, the state-operated forensic services program, or the Minnesota Sex Offender Program as:</p>
          <p>(1) a corrections officer 1;</p>
          <p>(2) a corrections officer 2;</p>
          <p>(3) a corrections officer 3;</p>
          <p>(4) a corrections officer supervisor;</p>
          <p>(5) a corrections lieutenant;</p>
          <p>(6) a corrections captain;</p>
          <p>(7) a security counselor;</p>
          <p>(8) a security counselor lead; or</p>
          <p>(9) a corrections canine officer.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Maintenance, correctional industry, and trades.</span></h2>
          <p>"Covered correctional service" also means service rendered at any time by state employees as maintenance personnel, correctional industry personnel, or members of trades certified by the commissioner of management and budget to the executive director as being engaged for at least 75 percent of the employee's working time in the rehabilitation, treatment, custody, or supervision of inmates at a Minnesota correctional facility, or of patients in the state-operated forensic services program or the Minnesota Sex Offender Program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Special teachers.</span></h2>
          <p>"Covered correctional service" also means service rendered by a state employee as a special teacher employed by the Department of Corrections or by Direct Care and Treatment at a security unit, provided that at least 75 percent of the employee's working time is spent in direct contact with inmates or patients and the fact of this direct contact is certified to the executive director by the appropriate commissioner or executive board, unless the person elects to retain the current retirement coverage under Laws 1996, chapter 408, article 8, section 21.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/408/#laws.8.29.0">1996 c 408 art 8 s 29</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.65.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 65</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.13.65.0">2015 c 68 art 13 s 65</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3c.<span class="headnote">Nursing personnel.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Covered correctional service" means service by a state employee in one of the employment positions at a correctional facility, in the state-operated forensic services program, or in the Minnesota Sex Offender Program that are specified in paragraph (b) if at least 75 percent of the employee's working time is spent in direct contact with inmates or patients and the fact of this direct contact is certified to the executive director by the appropriate commissioner or executive board.</p>
          <p>(b) The employment positions are as follows:</p>
          <p>(1) registered nurse - senior;</p>
          <p>(2) registered nurse;</p>
          <p>(3) registered nurse - principal;</p>
          <p>(4) licensed practical nurse;</p>
          <p>(5) registered nurse advance practice; and</p>
          <p>(6) psychiatric advance practice registered nurse.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3d">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3d." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3d">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3d.<span class="headnote">Other correctional personnel.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) "Covered correctional service" means service by a state employee in one of the employment positions at a correctional facility or in the state-operated forensic services program specified in paragraph (b) if at least 75 percent of the employee's working time is spent in direct contact with inmates or patients and the fact of this direct contact is certified to the executive director by the appropriate commissioner or executive board.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The employment positions are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) automotive mechanic;</p>
          <p>(2) baker;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) central services administrative specialist, intermediate;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) central services administrative specialist, principal;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) chaplain;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) chief cook;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) clinical program therapist 1;</p>
          <p>(8) clinical program therapist 2;</p>
          <p>(9) clinical program therapist 3;</p>
          <p>(10) clinical program therapist 4;</p>
          <p>(11) cook;</p>

          <p class="in">(14) corrections transitions program coordinator;</p>
          <p class="in">(15) corrections security caseworker;</p>

          <p class="in">(19) dentist;</p>

          <p class="in">(22) general repair worker;</p>
          <p class="in">(23) library/information research services specialist;</p>
          <p class="in">(24) library/information research services specialist senior;</p>
          <p class="in">(25) library technician;</p>
          <p class="in">(26) painter lead;</p>
          <p class="in">(27) plant maintenance engineer lead;</p>
          <p class="in">(28) plumber supervisor;</p>
          <p class="in">(29) psychologist 1;</p>
          <p class="in">(30) psychologist 3;</p>
          <p class="in">(31) recreation therapist;</p>
          <p class="in">(32) recreation therapist coordinator;</p>
          <p class="in">(33) recreation program assistant;</p>
          <p class="in">(34) recreation therapist senior;</p>
          <p class="in">(35) sports medicine specialist;</p>
          <p class="in">(36) work therapy assistant;</p>
          <p class="in">(37) work therapy program coordinator; and</p>

        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3e">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3e." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3e">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3e.<span class="headnote">Minnesota Specialty Health System-Cambridge.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) "Covered correctional service" means service by a state employee in one of the employment positions with the Minnesota Specialty Health System-Cambridge specified in paragraph (b) if at least 75 percent of the employee's working time is spent in direct contact with patients who are in the Minnesota Specialty Health System-Cambridge and if service in such a position is certified to the executive director by the Direct Care and Treatment executive board.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The employment positions are:</p>
          <p class="in">(1) behavior analyst 1;</p>
          <p class="in">(2) behavior analyst 2;</p>
          <p class="in">(3) behavior analyst 3;</p>
          <p class="in">(4) group supervisor;</p>
          <p class="in">(5) group supervisor assistant;</p>
          <p class="in">(6) human services support specialist;</p>
          <p class="in">(7) residential program lead;</p>
          <p class="in">(8) psychologist 2;</p>
          <p class="in">(9) recreation program assistant;</p>
          <p class="in">(10) recreation therapist senior;</p>
          <p class="in">(11) registered nurse senior;</p>
          <p class="in">(12) skills development specialist;</p>
          <p class="in">(13) social worker senior;</p>
          <p class="in">(14) social worker specialist; and</p>
          <p class="in">(15) speech pathology specialist.</p>
          <p>(c) A Department of Human Services or Direct Care and Treatment employee who was employed at the Minnesota Specialty Health System-Cambridge immediately preceding the 2014 conversion to the community-based homes and was in covered correctional service at the time of the transition shall continue to be covered by the correctional employees retirement plan while employed by and without a break in service with the Department of Human Services or Direct Care and Treatment in the direct care and treatment of patients.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3f">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3f." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3f">§</a>

          <p class="in">(a) "Covered correctional service" means service by a state employee in one of the employment positions specified in paragraph (b) in the state-operated forensic services program or the Minnesota Sex Offender Program if at least 75 percent of the employee's working time is spent in direct contact with patients and the determination of this direct contact is certified to the executive director by the commissioner of human services or Direct Care and Treatment executive board.</p>

          <p>(26) physical therapist;</p>
          <p class="in">(27) psychologist 1;</p>

          <p class="in">(29) psychologist 3;</p>

          <p class="in">(32) recreation therapist senior;</p>

          <p>(34) residential program lead;</p>

          <p class="in">(38) social worker specialist;</p>
          <p class="in">(39) social worker specialist, senior;</p>

        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3g">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3g." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3g">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3g.<span class="headnote">Additional Corrections Department personnel.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Covered correctional service" means service by a state employee in one of the employment positions specified in paragraph (b) if at least 75 percent of the employee's working time is spent in direct contact with inmates and the determination of this direct contact is certified to the executive director by the commissioner of corrections.</p>
          <p>(b) The qualifying employment positions are:</p>
          <p>(1) corrections discipline unit supervisor;</p>
          <p>(2) dental assistant registered;</p>
          <p>(3) dental hygienist;</p>
          <p>(4) food service supervisor;</p>
          <p>(5) medical assistant, certified;</p>
          <p>(6) psychologist 2; and</p>
          <p>(7) sentencing to service crew leader involved with the inmate community work crew program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3h">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3h." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3h">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3h.<span class="headnote">Employment occupation name changes.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) If the occupational title of a state employee covered by the Minnesota correctional employees retirement plan changes from the applicable title listed in subdivision 1, 2, 2a, 3c, 3d, 3e, 3f, or 3g, qualification for coverage by the correctional state employees retirement plan continues until the July 1 next following the title change if the commissioner of management and budget certifies to the executive director of the Minnesota State Retirement System and to the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement that the duties, requirements, and responsibilities of the new occupational title are substantially identical to the duties, requirements, and responsibilities of the prior occupational title.</p>
          <p>(b) If the commissioner of management and budget does not certify a new occupational title under paragraph (a), eligibility for future correctional state employees retirement coverage terminates as of the start of the first payroll period next following the effective date of the occupational title change.</p>
          <p>(c) For consideration by the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement during the legislative session next following an occupational title change involving a state employee in covered correctional service, the commissioner of management and budget shall submit the applicable draft proposed legislation reflecting the occupational title change covered by this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3i">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3i." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3i">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3i.<span class="headnote">Lateral transfers to new correctional facilities.</span></h2>
          <p>If a new correctional facility is established, a state employee rendering covered correctional service immediately before the transfer remains eligible for coverage by the correctional state employees retirement plan for future state employment at the new facility if the person is employed in the same occupational title at the new facility. The eligibility for future coverage continues until the July 1 next following the effective date of the establishment of the new facility.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.3j">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3j." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.3j">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3j.<span class="headnote">State-operated forensic services program.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this section, "state-operated forensic services program" means the Minnesota Security Hospital, the forensic nursing home, the forensic transition service, and the competency restoration program.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2000/0/461/#laws.6.6.0">2000 c 461 art 6 s 6</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Process for evaluating and recommending potential employment positions for membership inclusion.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The Department of Corrections and Direct Care and Treatment must establish a procedure for evaluating periodic requests by department and agency employees for qualification for recommendation by the commissioner or executive board for inclusion of the employment position in the correctional facility or Direct Care and Treatment facility in the correctional retirement plan and for periodically determining employment positions that no longer qualify for continued correctional retirement plan coverage.</p>
          <p>(b) The procedure must provide for an evaluation of the extent of the employee's working time spent in direct contact with patients or inmates, the extent of the physical hazard that the employee is routinely subjected to in the course of employment, and the extent of intervention routinely expected of the employee in the event of a facility incident. The percentage of routine direct contact with inmates or patients may not be less than 75 percent.</p>
          <p>(c) The applicable commissioner or executive board shall notify the employee of the determination of the appropriateness of recommending the employment position for inclusion in the correctional retirement plan, if the evaluation procedure results in a finding that the employee:</p>
          <p>(1) routinely spends 75 percent of the employee's time in direct contact with inmates or patients; and</p>
          <p>(2) is regularly engaged in the rehabilitation, treatment, custody, or supervision of inmates or patients.</p>
          <p>(d) After providing the affected employee an opportunity to dispute or clarify any evaluation determinations, if the commissioner or executive board determines that the employment position is appropriate for inclusion in the correctional retirement plan, the commissioner or executive board shall forward that recommendation and supporting documentation to the chair of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement, the chair of the State and Local Governmental Operations Committee of the senate, the chair of the Governmental Operations and Veterans Affairs Policy Committee of the house of representatives, and the executive director of the Legislative Commission on Pensions and Retirement in the form of the appropriate proposed legislation. The recommendation must be forwarded to the legislature before January 15 for the recommendation to be considered in that year's legislative session.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.4b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.4b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Department of Corrections; procedure for coverage change considerations.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The commissioner of corrections shall appoint a standing review committee to review and determine positions that should be included in legislative requests for correctional employees retirement plan coverage under subdivision 4a.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Periodically, the Department of Corrections will convene meetings of the review committee. The review committee must review all requests and the supporting documentation for coverage by the correctional employees retirement plan and must determine which classes or positions meet the statutory requirements for coverage. The review committee also must determine if incumbents of and recent retirees from classes or positions determined for inclusion in correctional employees retirement plan coverage have prior Department of Corrections employment which also qualified as correctional service and which should be transferred from the general state employees retirement plan to the plan and the initial date for each potential service credit transfer.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The review committee must evaluate and determine the eligibility date for initial plan participation and all periods of eligibility in the correctional employees retirement plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The department must provide a notice of each determination and of the employee's right to appeal from the review committee to each employee who requested inclusion. Appeals must be filed with the agency human resource manager within 30 days of the date of the notice of determination.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The commissioner of corrections shall appoint a standing appeals committee to hear appeals of determinations for coverage. The appeal committee must include relevant department employees and employee representatives. Appeal committee determinations are final.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) All positions approved for inclusion must be forwarded to the commissioner of corrections for the preparation of legislation to implement the coverage change and submission. The commissioner will submit a written recommendation documenting classes or positions that should or should not be covered by the correctional employees retirement plan. Documentation of each request and the final determination must be retained in the Department of Corrections' Office of Human Resource Management.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.4c">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4c." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.4c">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4c.<span class="headnote">Direct Care and Treatment; procedure for coverage change considerations.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The Direct Care and Treatment executive board shall appoint a standing review committee to review and determine classifications or positions that may be included in legislative requests for correctional state employees retirement plan coverage under subdivision 4a.</p>
          <p>(b) The agency's human resources director shall convene a meeting of the review committee only at the request of a labor organization or a member of the agency's management team.</p>
          <p>(c) The review committee must review all requests and the supporting documentation for coverage by the correctional state employees retirement plan and must make a recommendation to the executive board regarding which classifications or positions meet the statutory requirements for coverage. The review committee must also make a recommendation to the executive board regarding classifications or positions that no longer meet the statutory requirement for coverage by the correctional state employees retirement plan and removal of the classification or position from the applicable statute.</p>
          <p>(d) The agency's human resources director must provide a notice of each determination and of the employee's right to appeal the determination. Appeals must be filed with the agency's human resources director within 30 days of the date of the notice of determination.</p>
          <p>(e) The Direct Care and Treatment executive board shall review appeals of determinations for coverage. The executive board's determinations are final.</p>
          <p>(f) All classifications or positions recommended by the review committee for inclusion in or exclusion from the correctional state employees retirement plan must be forwarded to the Direct Care and Treatment executive board for the preparation of legislation to implement the coverage change and submission. If the executive board determines that the employment position is appropriate for inclusion in or exclusion from the correctional state employees retirement plan, the executive board shall submit a written recommendation documenting classifications or positions that should or should not be covered by the correctional state employees retirement plan. The agency's human resources director must retain the documentation of each request and the final determination.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.2.19.0">2010 c 359 art 2 s 19</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.91.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.91.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Correction of plan coverage errors.</span></h2>
          <p>If erroneous employee deductions and employer contributions are caused by an error in plan coverage involving the correctional state employees retirement plan and any other plan specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.637">356.637</a>, that section applies.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

art 11 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/372/#laws.1.4.0">1987 c 372 art 1 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/408/#laws.8.10.0">1996 c 408 art 8 s 10</a>-17; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.13.2.0">1999 c 222 art 13 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.3.1.0">2007 c 134 art 3 s 1</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/204/#laws.0.42.0">2008 c 204 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.6.1.0">2008 c 349 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.3.1.0">2009 c 169 art 3 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.2.5.0">2010 c 359 art 2 s 5</a>;
