<a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.15.2.0">2018 c 211 art 15 s 2</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/65/#laws.1.3.0">2022 c 65 art 1 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/98/#laws.4.51.0">2022 c 98 art 4 s 51</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/25/#laws.0.170.0">2023 c 25 s 170</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/79/#laws.9.14.0">2024 c 79 art 9 s 14</a>-19; art 10 s 3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/102/#laws.5.9.0">2024 c 102 art 5 s 9</a>; art 8 s 14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/125/#laws.5.42.0">2024 c 125 art 5 s 42</a>,43; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/127/#laws.50.42.0">2024 c 127 art 50 s 42</a>,43</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.911">
        <h1 class="shn">352.911 CORRECTIONAL EMPLOYEES RETIREMENT FUND.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.911.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.911.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Fund created.</span></h2>
          <p>There is created a special fund to be known as the correctional employees retirement fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.911.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.911.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Revenue sources.</span></h2>
          <p>Employee contributions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.92#stat.352.92.1">352.92, subdivision 1</a>, and employer contributions under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.92#stat.352.92.2">352.92, subdivision 2</a>, and other amounts, including any investment income on invested fund assets as authorized by law, shall be deposited in the fund.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.911.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.911.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Investment.</span></h2>
          <p> The assets of the fund must be deposited in the Minnesota combined investment funds as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/11A.14">11A.14</a>, if applicable, or otherwise under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/11A.23">11A.23</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.911.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.911.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Collection of contributions.</span></h2>
          <p>The collection of employee and employer contributions shall be governed by the applicable procedures set forth in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04">352.04, subdivisions 4, 5, 6, 8, and 9</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.911.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.911.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Fund disbursement restricted.</span></h2>
          <p>The correctional employees retirement fund must be disbursed only for the purposes provided for in the applicable provisions in this chapter. The proportional share of the expenses of the system and any benefits provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.90">352.90 </a>must be paid from the correctional employees retirement fund. The retirement allowances, retirement annuities, the disability benefits, the survivorship benefits, and any refunds of accumulated deductions must be paid from the correctional employees retirement fund. The amounts necessary to make the payments from the correctional employees retirement fund are annually appropriated from that fund for those purposes.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.911.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/259/#laws.0.20.0">1987 c 259 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.18.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 18</a>,19</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.92">
        <h1 class="shn">352.92 CORRECTIONAL EMPLOYEE CONTRIBUTIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.92.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.92.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Employee contributions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Employee contributions of covered correctional employees must be in an amount equal to the following percent of salary:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:7.692307692307693%">
            <col style="width:53.8461538461538%">
            <col style="width:38.4615384615385%">
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2014, to June 30, 2018</td>
              <td>9.1</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>after June 30, 2018</td>
              <td>9.6</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(b) These contributions must be made by deduction from salary as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.04#stat.352.04.4">352.04, subdivision 4</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) Contribution increases under paragraph (a) must be paid starting the first day of the first full pay period after the effective date of the increase.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.92.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.92.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Employer contributions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The employer shall contribute for covered correctional employees an amount equal to the following percent of salary:</p>
          <table>
            <col style="width:7.692307692307693%">
            <col style="width:53.8461538461538%">
            <col style="width:38.4615384615385%">
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>from July 1, 2014, to June 30, 2018</td>
              <td>12.85</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <td></td>
              <td>after June 30, 2018</td>
              <td>14.4</td>
            </tr>
          </table>
          <p>(b) Contribution increases under paragraph (a) must be paid starting the first day of the first full pay period after the effective date of the increase.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.92.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.92.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Supplemental employer contribution.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Effective July 1, 2019, the employer shall pay a supplemental contribution. The supplemental contribution is 1.45 percent of salary for covered correctional employees from July 1, 2019, through June 30, 2020; 2.95 percent of salary for covered correctional employees from July 1, 2020, through June 30, 2021; and 4.45 percent of salary for covered correctional employees thereafter. The supplemental contribution rate of 4.45 percent remains in effect until, for three consecutive years, the market value of the assets of the correctional state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System equals or exceeds the actuarial accrued liability of the plan as determined by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>. The expiration of the supplemental employer contribution is effective the first day of the first full pay period of the fiscal year immediately following the issuance of the third actuarial valuation upon which the expiration is based.</p>
          <p>(b) The supplemental contribution under paragraph (a) must be paid starting the first day of the first full pay period after June 30, 2018.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.92.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.92.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Plan administration.</span></h2>
          <p>The Minnesota State Retirement System shall administer the correctional employees retirement plan established by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.90">352.90</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.951">352.951</a> in accordance with this chapter and chapters 356 and 356A.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.92.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/653/#laws.0.41.0">1973 c 653 s 41</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/3/2/#laws.1.66.0">3Sp1981 c 2 art 1 s 66</a>,67; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1982/0/641/#laws.1.11.0">1982 c 641 art 1 s 11</a>,12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/564/#laws.0.13.0">1984 c 564 s 13</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.8.11.0">1989 c 319 art 8 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/591/#laws.2.3.0">1990 c 591 art 2 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/408/#laws.8.18.0">1996 c 408 art 8 s 18</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/233/#laws.1.21.0">1997 c 233 art 1 s 21</a>,22; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.13.3.0">1999 c 222 art 13 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.1.4.0">2006 c 271 art 1 s 4</a>,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.3.3.0">2014 c 296 art 3 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.7.3.0">2018 c 211 art 7 s 3</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2023/0/45/#laws.3.3.0">2023 c 45 art 3 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.925">
        <h1 class="shn">352.925 VESTING.</h1>
        <p>(a) "Vesting" means obtaining a nonforfeitable entitlement to an annuity or benefit from the correctional state employees retirement plan by having credit for sufficient allowable service under paragraph (b) or (c), whichever applies.</p>
        <p>(b) A member who first became a member of the correctional state employees retirement plan before July 1, 2010, is vested when the person has accrued credit for not less than three years of allowable service as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.11">352.01, subdivision 11</a>.</p>
        <p>(c) A member who first becomes a member of the correctional state employees retirement plan after June 30, 2010, is vested at the following percentages when the person has accrued credited allowable service as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.11">352.01, subdivision 11</a>, as follows:</p>
        <p>(i) 50 percent after five years;</p>
        <p>(ii) 60 percent after six years;</p>
        <p>(iii) 70 percent after seven years;</p>
        <p>(iv) 80 percent after eight years;</p>
        <p>(v) 90 percent after nine years; and</p>
        <p>(vi) 100 percent after ten years.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.925.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.10.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 10</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.93">
        <h1 class="shn">352.93 RETIREMENT ANNUITY.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.93.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.93.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Basis of annuity; when to apply.</span></h2>
          <p>After separation from state service, an employee covered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.91">352.91</a> who has reached age 55 years and is vested under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.925">352.925</a>, is entitled upon application to a retirement annuity under this section, based only on covered correctional employees' service. Application may be made no earlier than 60 days before the date the employee is eligible to retire by reason of both age and service requirements.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.93.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.93.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Calculating monthly annuity.</span></h2>
          <p>The monthly annuity under this section must be determined by multiplying the average monthly salary by the number of years, or completed months, of covered correctional service by 2.4 percent if employed as a correctional state employee before July 1, 2010, or 2.2 percent if employed as a correctional state employee after June 30, 2010.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.93.2a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.93.2a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2a.<span class="headnote">Early retirement.</span></h2>
          <p>Any covered correctional employee who becomes at least 50 years old and is vested under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.925">352.925</a>, is entitled upon application to a reduced retirement annuity equal to the annuity calculated under subdivision 2, reduced by two-tenths of one percent for each month that the correctional employee is under age 55 at the time of retirement if first employed as a correctional state employee before July 1, 2010, and if retired before July 1, 2015, or reduced by 0.417 percent for each month that the correctional employee is under age 55 at the time of retirement if first employed as a correctional state employee after June 30, 2010, or if first employed as a correctional state employee before July 1, 2010, and if retired after June 30, 2015.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.93.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.93.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Annuity accrual.</span></h2>
          <p>The annuity under this section must begin to accrue as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.115#stat.352.115.8">352.115, subdivision 8</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.93.3a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.93.3a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Optional annuities.</span></h2>
          <p>The board may establish optional annuity forms to pay a higher amount from the date of retirement until an employee is first eligible to draw Social Security benefits, reaches age 65, or reaches the age the employee is eligible to receive unreduced Social Security benefits, at which time the monthly benefits must be reduced. The optional annuity forms must be actuarially equivalent to the normal single life annuity form provided in subdivision 2. The optional annuity forms must be certified as actuarially equivalent by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.93.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.93.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Employee with regular and correctional service.</span></h2>
          <p>A former employee who has both regular and correctional service shall, if qualified, receive an annuity based on both periods of service under applicable sections of law but no period of service shall be used more than once in calculating the annuity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.93.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.93.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.36.0">1983 c 128 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.93.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.93.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.36.0">1983 c 128 s 36</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.93.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.93.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A retirement annuity under this section is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.93.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/653/#laws.0.42.0">1973 c 653 s 42</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/368/#laws.0.36.0">1975 c 368 s 36</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/607/#laws.14.46.0">1980 c 607 art 14 s 46</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.14.0">1983 c 128 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/564/#laws.0.14.0">1984 c 564 s 14</a>,15; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/372/#laws.9.6.0">1987 c 372 art 9 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.1.9.0">1989 c 319 art 1 s 9</a>; art 13 s 20,21; art 17 s 2,3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/570/#laws.12.11.0">1990 c 570 art 12 s 11</a>,12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.1.20.0">1993 c 307 art 1 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/528/#laws.1.8.0">1994 c 528 art 1 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/233/#laws.1.23.0">1997 c 233 art 1 s 23</a>-25; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1999/0/222/#laws.13.5.0">1999 c 222 art 13 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/392/#laws.11.52.0">2002 c 392 art 11 s 52</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/1/8/#laws.1.6.0">1Sp2005 c 8 art 1 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.3.47.0">2006 c 271 art 3 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.20.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.11.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 11</a>-13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.4.3.0">2013 c 111 art 4 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.931">
        <h1 class="shn">352.931 SURVIVOR BENEFITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.931.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.931.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Surviving spouse benefit.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) If the correctional employee was at least age 50, was vested under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.925">352.925</a>, and dies before an annuity or disability benefit has become payable, notwithstanding any designation of beneficiary to the contrary, the surviving spouse of the employee may elect to receive, in lieu of the refund under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.12#stat.352.12.1">352.12, subdivision 1</a>, an annuity for life equal to the joint and 100 percent survivor annuity which the employee could have qualified for had the employee terminated service on the date of death. The election may be made at any time after the date of death of the employee. The surviving spouse benefit begins to accrue as of the first of the month next following the date on which the application for the benefit was filed.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) If the employee was under age 50, dies, and was vested under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.925">352.925</a>, but did not yet qualify for retirement, the surviving spouse may elect to receive a 100 percent joint and survivor annuity based on the age of the employee and surviving spouse at the time of death. The annuity is payable using the early retirement reduction under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.93#stat.352.93.2a">352.93, subdivision 2a</a>, to age 50, and one-half of the early retirement reduction from age 50 to the age payment begins. The surviving spouse eligible for surviving spouse benefits under this paragraph may apply for the annuity at any time after the employee's death. Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.22#stat.352.22.3">352.22, subdivision 3</a>, and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.72#stat.352.72.2">352.72, subdivision 2</a>, apply to a deferred annuity or surviving spouse benefit payable under this subdivision.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The annuity must cease with the last payment received by the surviving spouse in the lifetime of the surviving spouse. Any employee may request in writing, with the signed consent of the spouse, that this subdivision not apply and that payment be made only to a designated beneficiary as otherwise provided by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.931.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.931.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Surviving spouse coverage; term certain.</span></h2>
          <p>In lieu of the 100 percent optional annuity under subdivision 1, the surviving spouse of a deceased employee may elect to receive survivor coverage in a term certain of ten, 15, or 20 years. The monthly term certain annuity must be actuarially equivalent to the 100 percent optional annuity under subdivision 1 and must be approved by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>. The optional annuity ceases upon the expiration of the term certain period. If a survivor elects a term certain annuity and dies before the expiration of the specified term certain period, the commuted value of the remaining annuity payments must be paid in a lump sum to the survivor's estate.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.931.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.931.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Dependent child survivor coverage.</span></h2>
          <p>If there is no surviving spouse eligible for benefits under subdivision 1, a dependent child as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.26">352.01, subdivision 26</a>, is eligible for a dependent child survivor benefit. Benefits to a dependent child must be paid from the date of the employee's death to the date the dependent child attains age 20 if the child is under age 15 on the date of death. If the child is 15 years or older on the date of death, the benefit is payable for five years. The payment to a dependent child is an amount actuarially equivalent to the value of a 100 percent joint and survivor optional annuity using the age of the employee and age of the dependent child at the date of death in lieu of the age of the surviving spouse. If there is more than one dependent child, each dependent child shall receive a proportionate share of the actuarial value of the employee's account, with the amount of the benefit payable to each child to be determined based on the portion of the total eligibility period that each child is eligible. The process for calculating the dependent child survivor benefit must be approved by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.931.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.931.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Death refund.</span></h2>
          <p>An amount equal to the excess, if any, of the accumulated contributions credited to the account of the deceased employee in excess of the total of the benefits paid to the surviving spouse and surviving child or children must be paid to the deceased employee's last designated beneficiary or, if none, as specified under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.12#stat.352.12.1">352.12, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.931.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.931.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>The benefit elections under this section must be made on an application form prescribed by the executive director and must be filed with the executive director.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.931.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.931.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A survivor benefit under this section is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.931.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/233/#laws.1.26.0">1997 c 233 art 1 s 26</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2006/0/271/#laws.3.47.0">2006 c 271 art 3 s 47</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.4.2.0">2008 c 349 art 4 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.21.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.14.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 14</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.94">
        <h1 class="shn">352.94 AUGMENTATION FOR EMPLOYEES WITH GENERAL AND CORRECTIONAL SERVICE.</h1>
        <p>An employee who becomes covered by the correctional plan after serving as a general plan covered employee, or becomes covered by the general plan after serving as a correctional plan covered employee, is covered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.311">356.311</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.94.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/653/#laws.0.43.0">1973 c 653 s 43</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.1.21.0">1993 c 307 art 1 s 21</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2022/0/65/#laws.9.2.0">2022 c 65 art 9 s 2</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.95">
        <h1 class="shn">352.95 DISABILITY BENEFITS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.95.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.95.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Duty disability; computation of benefit.</span></h2>
          <p>A covered correctional employee who is determined to have a duty disability, physical or psychological, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17b">352.01, subdivision 17b</a>, is entitled to a duty disability benefit. The duty disability benefit must be based on covered correctional service only. The duty disability benefit amount is 50 percent of the average salary defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.93">352.93</a>, plus an additional 2.4 percent if employed as a correctional state employee before July 1, 2010, or 2.2 percent if employed as a correctional state employee after June 30, 2010, for each year of covered correctional service in excess of 20 years, ten months, prorated for completed months.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.95.1a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 1a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.95.1a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 1a.<span class="headnote">Optional annuity election.</span></h2>
          <p>A disabled correctional employee may elect the normal disability benefit or an optional annuity as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.3">352.116, subdivision 3</a>. The election of an optional annuity must be made before commencement of payment of the disability benefit and becomes effective the date on which the disability benefit begins to accrue as provided in subdivision 3. Upon becoming effective, the optional annuity shall begin to accrue on the same date as provided for the disability benefit.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.95.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.95.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Regular disability; computation of benefit.</span></h2>
          <p>A covered correctional employee who was hired before July 1, 2009, after rendering at least one year of covered correctional service, or a covered correctional employee who was first hired after June 30, 2009, is vested under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.925">352.925</a>, and who is determined to have a regular disability, physical or psychological, as defined under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17c">352.01, subdivision 17c</a>, is entitled to a regular disability benefit. The regular disability benefit must be based on covered correctional service only. The regular disability benefit must be computed as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.93">352.93, subdivisions 1</a> and 2. The regular disability benefit of a covered correctional employee who was first hired before July 1, 2009, and who is determined to have a regular disability, physical or psychological, under this subdivision must be computed as though the employee had at least 15 years of covered correctional service.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.95.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.95.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Applying for benefits; accrual.</span></h2>
          <p>No application for disability benefits may be made until after the last day physically on the job. The disability benefit begins to accrue the day following the last day for which the employee is paid sick leave or annual leave, but not earlier than 180 days before the date the application is filed. A terminated employee must file a written application in an office of the system or with a person authorized by the executive director. Applications must comply with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113#stat.352.113.2">352.113, subdivision 2</a>, paragraph (b).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.95.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.95.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Medical or psychological evidence.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) An applicant shall provide medical, chiropractic, or psychological evidence to support an application for disability benefits. The director may have the employee examined by at least one additional licensed physician, APRN, chiropractor, or psychologist who is designated by the medical adviser. The physicians, APRNs, chiropractors, or psychologists with respect to a mental impairment, shall make written reports to the director concerning the question of the employee's disability, including their expert opinions as to whether the employee has an occupational disability within the meaning of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17a">352.01, subdivision 17a</a>, and whether the employee has a duty disability, physical or psychological, under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17b">352.01, subdivision 17b</a>, or has a regular disability, physical or psychological, under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17c">352.01, subdivision 17c</a>. The director shall also obtain written certification from the employer stating whether or not the employee is on sick leave of absence because of a disability that will prevent further service to the employer performing normal duties as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17d">352.01, subdivision 17d</a>, or performing less frequent duties as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17e">352.01, subdivision 17e</a>, and as a consequence, the employee is not entitled to compensation from the employer.</p>
          <p>(b) If, on considering the reports by the physicians, APRNs, chiropractors, or psychologists and any other evidence supplied by the employee or others, the medical adviser finds that the employee has an occupational disability within the meaning of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.01#stat.352.01.17a">352.01, subdivision 17a</a>, the adviser shall make the appropriate recommendation to the director, in writing, together with the date from which the employee has been disabled. The director shall then determine the propriety of authorizing payment of a duty disability benefit or a regular disability benefit as provided in this section.</p>
          <p>(c) Unless the payment of a disability benefit has terminated because the employee no longer has an occupational disability, or because the employee has reached either age 55 or the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later, the disability benefit must cease with the last payment which was received by the disabled employee or which had accrued during the employee's lifetime. While disability benefits are paid, the director has the right, at reasonable times, to require the disabled employee to submit proof of the continuance of an occupational disability. If any examination indicates to the medical adviser that the employee no longer has an occupational disability, the disability payment must be discontinued upon the person's reinstatement to state service or within 60 days of the finding, whichever is sooner.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.95.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.95.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Retirement status at normal retirement age.</span></h2>
          <p>The disability benefit paid to a disabled correctional employee under this section terminates at the end of the month in which the employee reaches age 55, or the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later. If the disabled correctional employee is still disabled when the employee reaches age 55, or the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later, the employee must be deemed to be a retired employee. If the employee had elected an optional annuity under subdivision 1a, the employee shall receive an annuity in accordance with the terms of the optional annuity previously elected. If the employee had not elected an optional annuity under subdivision 1a, the employee may within 90 days of attaining age 55 or reaching the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later, either elect to receive a normal retirement annuity computed in the manner provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.93">352.93</a> or elect to receive an optional annuity as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.116#stat.352.116.3">352.116, subdivision 3</a>, based on the same length of service as used in the calculation of the disability benefit. Election of an optional annuity must be made within 90 days before attaining age 55 or reaching the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later. If an optional annuity is elected, the optional annuity begins to accrue on the first of the month following the month in which the employee reaches age 55 or the five-year anniversary of the effective date of the disability benefit, whichever is later.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.95.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.95.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Disability benefit for certain employees with regular plan service.</span></h2>
          <p>If the employee is entitled to receive a disability benefit as provided in subdivision 1 or 2 and has credit for less covered correctional service than the length of service upon which the correctional disability benefit is based, and also has credit for regular plan service, the employee is entitled to a disability benefit or deferred retirement annuity based on the regular plan service only for that service which when combined with the correctional service exceeds number of years on which the correctional disability benefit is based. The disabled employee who also has credit for regular plan service must in all respects qualify under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.113">352.113 </a>for a disability benefit based on the regular plan service, except that the service may be combined to satisfy length of service requirements. Any deferred annuity to which the employee is entitled based on regular plan service must be augmented as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.22#stat.352.22.3a">352.22, subdivision 3a</a>, while the employee is receiving a disability benefit under this section.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.95.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.95.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Resumption of employment.</span></h2>
          <p>If the disabled employee resumes gainful work, the disability benefit must be continued in an amount which when added to current earnings and workers' compensation benefits does not exceed the salary rate of the disabled employee at the date of disability as adjusted by the same percentage increase in United States average wages used by Social Security in calculating average indexed monthly earnings for the same period.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.95.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.95.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Postretirement adjustment eligibility.</span></h2>
          <p>A disability benefit under this section is eligible for postretirement adjustments under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.415">356.415</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.95.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1973/0/653/#laws.0.44.0">1973 c 653 s 44</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1980/0/342/#laws.0.6.0">1980 c 342 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/68/#laws.0.12.0">1981 c 68 s 12</a>,13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1983/0/128/#laws.0.15.0">1983 c 128 s 15</a>,16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/564/#laws.0.16.0">1984 c 564 s 16</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1984/0/574/#laws.0.9.0">1984 c 574 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1985/1/7/#laws.0.7.0">1Sp1985 c 7 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1986/0/444/#laws.0.0.0">1986 c 444</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/319/#laws.13.22.0">1989 c 319 art 13 s 22</a>,23; art 17 s 4,5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.1.22.0">1993 c 307 art 1 s 22</a>; art 2 s 6-8; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/465/#laws.3.30.0">1994 c 465 art 3 s 30</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1996/0/438/#laws.2.2.0">1996 c 438 art 2 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/233/#laws.1.27.0">1997 c 233 art 1 s 27</a>,28; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/169/#laws.1.22.0">2009 c 169 art 1 s 22</a>; art 2 s 10-14; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.1.15.0">2010 c 359 art 1 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.4.4.0">2013 c 111 art 4 s 4</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/275/#laws.2.25.0">2014 c 275 art 2 s 25</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2020/0/108/#laws.5.9.0">2020 c 108 art 5 s 9</a>;       </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.951">
        <h1 class="shn">352.951 APPLICABILITY OF GENERAL LAW.</h1>
        <p class="in">Except as otherwise provided, this chapter applies to covered correctional employees, military affairs personnel covered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.85">352.85</a>, Transportation Department pilots covered under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.86">352.86</a>, and state fire marshal employees under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.87">352.87</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.951.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1981/0/224/#laws.0.60.0">1981 c 224 s 60</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1993/0/307/#laws.1.23.0">1993 c 307 art 1 s 23</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.8.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.955">
        <h1 class="shn">352.955 TRANSFER OF PRIOR MSRS-GENERAL SERVICE CREDIT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.955.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.955.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Election to transfer prior MSRS-general service credit.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An eligible employee described in paragraph (b) may elect to transfer service credit in the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System to the correctional state employees retirement plan for eligible prior correctional employment.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) An eligible employee is a person who is covered by legislation implementing the recommendations under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.91#stat.352.91.4a">352.91, subdivision 4a</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) Eligible prior correctional employment is employment covered by the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System, is continuous service, and is certified by the commissioner of corrections and the Direct Care and Treatment executive board, whichever applies, and by the commissioner of management and budget to the executive director of the Minnesota State Retirement System as service that would qualify for correctional state employees retirement plan coverage under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.91">352.91</a>, if the service had been rendered after the date of coverage transfer.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The election to transfer past service credit under this section must be made in writing by the applicable person on a form prescribed by the executive director of the Minnesota State Retirement System and must be filed with the executive director of the Minnesota State Retirement System on or before the one year anniversary of the coverage transfer or the date of the eligible employee's termination of state employment, whichever is earlier.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.955.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.955.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.2.33.0">2013 c 111 art 2 s 33</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.955.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.955.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Payment of additional equivalent contributions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) An eligible employee who is transferred to plan coverage and who elects to transfer past service credit under this section must pay an additional member contribution for that prior service period. The additional member contribution is the amount computed under paragraph (b), plus the greater of the amount computed under paragraph (c), or 40 percent of the unfunded actuarial accrued liability attributable to the past service credit transfer.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The executive director shall compute, for the most recent 12 months of service credit eligible for transfer, or for the entire period eligible for transfer if less than 12 months, the difference between the employee contribution rate or rates for the general state employees retirement plan and the employee contribution rate or rates for the correctional state employees retirement plan applied to the eligible employee's salary during that transfer period, plus compound interest at the applicable monthly rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.2">356.59, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The executive director shall compute, for any service credit being transferred on behalf of the eligible employee and not included under paragraph (b), the difference between the employee contribution rate or rates for the general state employees retirement plan and the employee contribution rate or rates for the correctional state employees retirement plan applied to the eligible employee's salary during that transfer period, plus compound interest at the applicable monthly rate or rates specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.59#stat.356.59.2">356.59, subdivision 2</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The executive director shall compute an amount using the process specified in paragraph (b), but based on differences in employer contribution rates between the general state employees retirement plan and the correctional state employees retirement plan rather than employee contribution rates.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The executive director shall compute an amount using the process specified in paragraph (c), but based on differences in employer contribution rates between the general state employees retirement plan and the correctional state employees retirement plan rather than employee contribution rates.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) The additional equivalent member contribution under this subdivision must be paid in a lump sum. Payment must accompany the election to transfer the prior service credit. No transfer election or additional equivalent member contribution payment may be made by a person or accepted by the executive director after the one year anniversary date of the effective date of the retirement coverage transfer, or the date on which the eligible employee terminates state employment, whichever is earlier.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) If an eligible employee elects to transfer past service credit under this section and pays the additional equivalent member contribution amount under paragraph (a), the applicable department shall pay an additional equivalent employer contribution amount. The additional employer contribution is the amount computed under paragraph (d), plus the greater of the amount computed under paragraph (e), or 60 percent of the unfunded actuarial accrued liability attributable to the past service credit transfer.</p>
          <p class="in">(h) The unfunded actuarial accrued liability attributable to the past service credit transfer is the present value of the benefit obtained by the transfer of the service credit to the correctional state employees retirement plan reduced by the amount of the asset transfer under subdivision 4, by the amount of the member contribution equivalent payment computed under paragraph (b), and by the amount of the employer contribution equivalent payment computed under paragraph (d).</p>
          <p class="in">(i) The additional equivalent employer contribution under this subdivision must be paid in a lump sum and must be paid within 30 days of the date on which the executive director of the Minnesota State Retirement System certifies to the applicable department that the employee paid the additional equivalent member contribution.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.955.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.955.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Transfer of assets.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Assets related to the transferred service credit of an eligible employee must be transferred from the general state employees retirement fund to the correctional state employees retirement fund in an amount equal to the present value of benefits earned under the general state employees retirement plan by the eligible employee transferring past service to the correctional state employees retirement plan, as determined by the actuary retained under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.214">356.214</a>, multiplied by the accrued liability funding ratio of the active members of the general state employees retirement plan as derived from the most recent actuarial valuation prepared under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.215">356.215</a>. The transfer of assets must be made within 45 days after the coverage transfer election is made.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.955.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.955.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Effect of asset transfer.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Upon the transfer of assets under subdivision 4, the service credit in the general state employees retirement plan of the Minnesota State Retirement System related to the period being transferred is forfeited and may not be reinstated. The transferred service credit and the transferred assets must be credited to the correctional state employees retirement plan and fund, respectively.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.955.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.955.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Cost of actuarial calculations.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The applicable department shall pay the cost of the actuarial calculations required by this section as billed by the executive director of the Minnesota State Retirement System.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.955.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.3.5.0">2007 c 134 art 3 s 5</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/148/#laws.2.83.0">2007 c 148 art 2 s 83</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.6.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 6</a>,7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/101/#laws.2.109.0">2009 c 101 art 2 s 109</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2013/0/111/#laws.2.19.0">2013 c 111 art 2 s 19</a>,20; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2015/0/68/#laws.3.3.0">2015 c 68 art 3 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2018/0/211/#laws.6.8.0">2018 c 211 art 6 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2024/0/79/#laws.10.3.0">2024 c 79 art 10 s 3</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">DEFERRED COMPENSATION</h1>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.352.96"><b>352.96</b> [Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.11.12.0">2008 c 349 art 11 s 12</a>]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.965">
        <h1 class="shn">352.965 MINNESOTA STATE DEFERRED COMPENSATION PLAN.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Establishment.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The Minnesota deferred compensation plan is established. For purposes of this section, "plan" means the Minnesota deferred compensation plan, unless the context clearly indicates otherwise. The Minnesota State Retirement System shall administer the plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The purpose of the plan is to provide a means for a public employee to contribute a portion of the employee's compensation to a tax-deferred investment account. The plan is an eligible tax-deferred compensation plan under section 457(b) of the Internal Revenue Code, United States Code, title 26, section 457(b), and the applicable regulations under Code of Federal Regulations, title 26, parts 1.457-3 to 1.457-10.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The board of directors of the Minnesota State Retirement System is the plan trustee and plan sponsor. The board's executive director is the plan administrator. Fiduciary activities of the plan must be undertaken in a manner consistent with chapter 356A.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The executive director, with the approval of the board of directors, shall adopt and amend, as required to maintain tax-qualified status, a written plan document specifying the material terms and conditions for eligibility, benefits, applicable limitations, and the time and form under which benefit distributions can be made. With the approval of the board of directors, the executive director may also establish policies and procedures necessary for the administration of the deferred compensation plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(e) The plan document must include provisions that are necessary to cause the plan to be an eligible deferred compensation plan within the meaning of section 457(b) of the Internal Revenue Code. The plan document may provide additional administrative and substantive provisions consistent with state law, provided that those provisions do not cause the plan to fail to be an eligible deferred compensation plan within the meaning of section 457(b) of the Internal Revenue Code and may include provisions for certain optional features and services.</p>
          <p class="in">(f) The board of directors may authorize the executive director to establish and administer a Roth 457 plan if authorized by the Internal Revenue Code or a Roth individual retirement account as defined under section 408A of the Internal Revenue Code.</p>
          <p class="in">(g) All amounts contributed to the deferred compensation plan and all earnings on those amounts must be held in trust, in custodial accounts, or in qualifying annuity contracts for the exclusive benefit of the plan participants and beneficiaries, as required by section 457(g) of the Internal Revenue Code and in accordance with sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.001">356.001</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356A.06#stat.356A.06.1">356A.06, subdivision 1</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(h) The information and data maintained in the accounts of the participants and beneficiaries are private data and must not be disclosed to anyone other than the participant or beneficiary pursuant to a court order or under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.49">356.49</a>.</p>
          <p class="in">(i) The plan document is not subject to the rule adoption process under the Administrative Procedure Act, including section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/14.386">14.386</a>, but must conform with applicable federal and state laws.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Right to participate in deferred compensation plan.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) At the request of an officer or employee of the state, an officer or employee of a political subdivision, or an employee covered by a retirement fund in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20#stat.356.20.2">356.20, subdivision 2</a>, the appointing authority shall defer the payment of part of the compensation of the public officer or employee through payroll deduction.</p>
          <p>(b) The amount to be deferred must be as provided in an agreement between the officer or employee and the plan sponsor. The agreement must be in a form specified by the executive director of the Minnesota State Retirement System and must be consistent with the requirements for an eligible plan under federal and state tax laws, regulations, and rulings.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Failure to implement plan.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The public employer must complete implementation of the deferred compensation plan within 45 days of the request as provided in subdivision 2. If the public employer fails to implement the deferred compensation plan, the public employer may not defer compensation under any existing or new deferred compensation plan from the date of the request until the date on which the deferred compensation plan provided for in this section is implemented. Upon the petition of a public officer or employee, the executive director of the Minnesota State Retirement System may order the public officer's or employee's public employer to implement the deferred compensation plan provided for in this section and may enforce that order in appropriate legal proceedings.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Plan investments.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Available investments are those investments chosen by the State Board of Investment under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.645">356.645</a> for the plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.4a">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4a." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.4a">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4a.<span class="headnote">Exclusive benefit.</span></h2>
          <p>All amounts contributed to the deferred compensation plan and all earnings on those amounts must be held for the exclusive benefit of the plan participants and beneficiaries. These amounts must be held in trust, in custodial accounts, or in qualifying annuity contracts as required by federal law in accordance with section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356A.06">356A.06</a>, subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.4b">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4b." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.4b">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4b.<span class="headnote">Employer contribution prohibition.</span></h2>
          <p>Except as authorized in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.24#stat.356.24.1">356.24, subdivision 1</a>, clause (5), employer contributions are prohibited.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.13.28.0">2014 c 296 art 13 s 28</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Plan administrative expenses.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The reasonable and necessary administrative expenses of the deferred compensation plan may be charged to plan participants in the form of an annual fee, an asset-based fee, a percentage of the contributions to the plan, or a combination thereof, as set forth in the plan document. The executive director of the system at the direction of the board of directors shall establish procedures to carry out this section including allocation of administrative costs of the plan to participants. Processes and procedures shall be set forth in the plan document. Fees cannot be charged on contributions and investment returns attributable to contributions made to the Minnesota supplemental investment funds before July 1, 1992.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) The plan document must conform to federal and state tax laws, regulations, and rulings, and is not subject to the Administrative Procedure Act.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) The executive director may contract with a third party to perform administrative and record keeping functions. The executive director may solicit bids and negotiate such contracts. Participating employers must provide the necessary data to the third-party record keeper as determined by the executive director. The third-party record keeper and the Minnesota State Retirement System shall follow the data privacy provisions under chapter 13. The third-party record keeper may not solicit participants for any product or services not related to the deferred compensation plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(d) The board of directors may authorize a third-party investment consultant to provide investment information and advice if the offering of such information and advice is consistent with the investment advice requirements applicable to private plans under Title VI, subtitle A, of the Pension Protection Act of 2006, Public Law 109-280, section 601.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Other laws not applicable.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Except as provided in this section, no provisions of this chapter or other law specifically referring to this chapter applies to this section unless the Minnesota deferred compensation plan is specifically referenced.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Exemption from process.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">No amount of deferred compensation is assignable or subject to execution, levy, attachment, garnishment, or other legal process, except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.58">518.58</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.581">518.581</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518A.53">518A.53</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.965.9">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 9." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.965.9">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Missing participants.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The plan document shall establish procedures to assist in locating participants. If a participant cannot be located the participant's benefits shall be deemed abandoned and the provisions of section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.65">356.65</a> shall apply to their disposition.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.965.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.11.3.0">2008 c 349 art 11 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/359/#laws.2.6.0">2010 c 359 art 2 s 6</a>,7; art 3 s 1; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.13.5.0">2014 c 296 art 13 s 5</a>-7</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.97">
        <h1 class="shn">352.97 PRIOR DEFERRED COMPENSATION PLANS; CONSTRUCTION.</h1>
        <p class="in">Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.965">352.965</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/352.97">352.97</a> do not preempt, prohibit, ratify, or approve any other deferred compensation plan established before or after June 3, 1975.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.97.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1975/0/273/#laws.0.2.0">1975 c 273 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1987/0/229/#laws.6.1.0">1987 c 229 art 6 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.11.4.0">2008 c 349 art 11 s 4</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">POSTRETIREMENT HEALTH CARE SAVINGS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.352.98">
        <h1 class="shn">352.98 HEALTH CARE SAVINGS PLAN.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.98.1">
          <a title="Link to Subdivision 1." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.98.1">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Plan created.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">This section must be administered by the executive director of the system with the advice and consent of the board of directors. The executive director shall establish a plan or plans, known as health care savings plans, through which an officer or employee of the state or of a political subdivision, including officers or employees covered by a plan or fund specified in chapter 353D, 354B, 354D, 424A, or section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.20#stat.356.20.2">356.20, subdivision 2</a>, may save to cover health care costs. For purposes of this section, a volunteer firefighter is an employee. The executive director shall make available one or more trusts, including a governmental trust or governmental trusts, authorized under the Internal Revenue Code to be eligible for tax-preferred or tax-free treatment through which employers and employees can save to cover health care costs.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.98.2">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 2." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.98.2">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Contracting authorized.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) The executive director shall administer the plan and contract with public and private entities to provide investment services, record keeping, benefit payments, and other functions necessary for the administration of the plan.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) As specified in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.645">356.645</a>, the State Board of Investment shall determine an appropriate selection of investment options that shall be offered by the health care savings plan.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.98.3">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 3." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.98.3">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Contributions.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">(a) Contributions to the plan must be defined in a personnel policy or in a collective bargaining agreement of a public employer or political subdivision. The executive director may offer different types of trusts permitted under the Internal Revenue Code to best meet the needs of different employer units.</p>
          <p class="in">(b) Contributions to the plan by or on behalf of the participant must be held in trust for reimbursement of eligible health-related expenses for participants and their dependents following termination from public employment or in other circumstances set forth in the plan document. The executive director shall maintain a separate account of the contributions made by or on behalf of each participant and the earnings thereon. The executive director shall make available a limited range of investment options, and each participant may direct the investment of the accumulations in the participant's account among the investment options made available by the executive director.</p>
          <p class="in">(c) This section does not obligate a public employer to meet and negotiate in good faith with the exclusive bargaining representative of any public employee group regarding an employer contribution to a postretirement or active employee health care savings plan authorized by this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/356.24#stat.356.24.1">356.24, subdivision 1</a>, clause (7). It is not the intent of the legislature to authorize the state to incur new funding obligations for the costs of retiree health care or the costs of administering retiree health care plans or accounts.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.98.4">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 4." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.98.4">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Reimbursement for health-related expenses.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The executive director shall reimburse participants at least quarterly for eligible health-related expenses, as allowable by federal and state law, until the participant exhausts the accumulation in the participant's account. If a participant dies prior to exhausting the participant's account balance, the participant's spouse or dependents are eligible to be reimbursed for health care expenses from the account until the account balance is exhausted. If an account balance remains after the death of a participant and all of the participant's legal dependents, the remainder of the account must be paid to the participant's beneficiaries or, if none, to a living person or persons named by the personal representative of the estate. The person or persons named must use the account for reimbursement of allowable health care expenses.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.98.5">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 5." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.98.5">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Fees.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">The executive director is authorized to charge fees to participants to cover the ongoing cost of operating the plan. The fees must be deposited in an administrative fee account. On January 1, following the end of the prior fiscal year, the executive director shall estimate the amount needed to cover plan expenses, record keeping costs, and custodial fees for the new fiscal year. If the balance of the administrative fee account is in excess of this amount, the excess must revert to participant accounts, or plan fees must be reduced to eliminate the excess, or the executive director may use a combination of both approaches to eliminate the excess.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.98.6">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 6." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.98.6">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/133/#laws.2.13.0">2007 c 133 art 2 s 13</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.98.7">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 7." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.98.7">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Contracting with private entities.</span></h2>
          <p>Nothing in this section prohibits employers from contracting with private entities to provide for health care reimbursement plans.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.352.98.8">
          <a title="Link to Subd. 8." class="permalink" href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/2024/cite/352/full#stat.352.98.8">§</a>
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Exemption from process.</span></h2>
          <p class="in">Assets in a health care savings plan account described in this section must be used for the reimbursement of health care expenses and are not assignable or subject to execution, levy, attachment, garnishment, or other legal process, except as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.58">518.58</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518.581">518.581</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/518A.53">518A.53</a>.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.352.98.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/10/#laws.7.1.0">1Sp2001 c 10 art 7 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/267/#laws.11.1.0">2004 c 267 art 11 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2007/0/134/#laws.2.9.0">2007 c 134 art 2 s 9</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/349/#laws.5.8.0">2008 c 349 art 5 s 8</a>-12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2012/0/286/#laws.4.1.0">2012 c 286 art 4 s 1</a>-4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/296/#laws.13.8.0">2014 c 296 art 13 s 8</a></p>
      </div>
